Build a **bouncing-ball physics playground** in a single self-contained HTML


file. Dark theme, centered canvas, satisfying to click. The graded contract:





**Layout**


- `#sim` — the simulation canvas, rendered at **at least 400×400 CSS pixels**.


- Buttons: `#drop` (drop a new ball), `#play` (toggle animation), `#clear`


  (remove all balls).


- Clicking the canvas adds a ball at the cursor.





**Physics** — balls are circles with position `(x,y)`, velocity `(vx,vy)`, and a


radius `r`:


- **Gravity** accelerates every ball downward each step (increase `vy`).


- A ball **bounces** off the four canvas edges: when it reaches an edge, clamp


  it inside and reverse the perpendicular velocity component (a little energy


  loss is fine, i.e. multiply by a restitution < 1).


- Balls must **stay within the canvas** — never tunnel through an edge.





**Programmatic API — required for grading.** Expose on `window`:





```js


window.sim = {


  w, h,                          // canvas size in pixels


  balls(),                       // -> [{x, y, vx, vy, r}, ...]


  addBall(x, y, vx=0, vy=0, r?), // add a ball


  clear(),                       // remove all balls


  step(dt=1),                    // advance the simulation one step (and redraw)


};


```





Grading drives this API: it releases a ball and checks it accelerates down,


bounces off the floor (velocity reverses, ball stays in bounds), bounces off a


wall, and that balls never escape the canvas over a long run. Balls must be


visibly drawn.

ling-3.0-flash — 1.000 · 8/8 tests passed (rescored) · 20.1s · 7,532 tok out · 5,286 think 
```html


<!DOCTYPE html>


<html lang="en">


<head>


<meta charset="UTF-8">


<title>Bouncing Ball Playground</title>


<style>


  * { box-sizing: border-box; }


  body {


    margin: 0;


    background: #111;


    color: #eee;


    font-family: system-ui, sans-serif;


    display: flex;


    flex-direction: column;


    align-items: center;


    justify-content: center;


    min-height: 100vh;


  }


  #controls {


    margin-bottom: 12px;


    display: flex;


    gap: 8px;


  }


  button {


    padding: 8px 18px;


    background: #2a2a3a;


    color: #eee;


    border: 1px solid #444;


    border-radius: 6px;


    cursor: pointer;


    font-size: 14px;


    transition: background 0.15s;


  }


  button:hover { background: #3a3a4a; }


  #sim {


    display: block;


    background: #1a1a2e;


    border: 2px solid #333;


    border-radius: 8px;


    cursor: pointer;


    width: 600px;


    height: 600px;


  }


</style>


</head>


<body>


<div id="controls">


  <button id="drop">Drop</button>


  <button id="play">Play</button>


  <button id="clear">Clear</button>


</div>


<canvas id="sim" width="600" height="600"></canvas>


<script>


(function() {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const W = canvas.width;


  const H = canvas.height;





  const gravity = 0.5;


  const restitution = 0.8;


  let balls = [];


  let animating = false;


  let rafId = null;





  function draw() {


    ctx.fillStyle = '#1a1a2e';


    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);


    for (const b of balls) {


      ctx.save();


      ctx.shadowColor = b.color;


      ctx.shadowBlur = 12;


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


      ctx.fillStyle = b.color;


      ctx.fill();


      ctx.restore();


      // highlight


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.arc(b.x - b.r * 0.3, b.y - b.r * 0.3, b.r * 0.25, 0, Math.PI * 2);


      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)';


      ctx.fill();


    }


  }





  function addBall(x, y, vx = 0, vy = 0, r = 10) {


    const hue = Math.random() * 360;


    balls.push({ x, y, vx, vy, r, color: `hsl(${hue},80%,60%)` });


  }





  function clearBalls() {


    balls.length = 0;


  }





  function step(dt = 1) {


    for (const b of balls) {


      b.vy += gravity * dt;


      b.x += b.vx * dt;


      b.y += b.vy * dt;





      if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


        b.x = b.r;


        if (b.vx < 0) b.vx = -b.vx * restitution;


      }


      if (b.x + b.r > W) {


        b.x = W - b.r;


        if (b.vx > 0) b.vx = -b.vx * restitution;


      }


      if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


        b.y = b.r;


        if (b.vy < 0) b.vy = -b.vy * restitution;


      }


      if (b.y + b.r > H) {


        b.y = H - b.r;


        if (b.vy > 0) b.vy = -b.vy * restitution;


      }


    }


    draw();


  }





  function loop() {


    if (!animating) return;


    step(1);


    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(loop);


  }





  // Expose API


  window.sim = {


    get w() { return W; },


    get h() { return H; },


    balls() {


      return balls.map(b => ({ x: b.x, y: b.y, vx: b.vx, vy: b.vy, r: b.r }));


    },


    addBall,


    clear: clearBalls,


    step,


  };





  // Button handlers


  document.getElementById('drop').addEventListener('click', () => {


    addBall(W / 2, 20, 0, 0, 12);


    if (!animating) draw();


  });





  document.getElementById('play').addEventListener('click', () => {


    animating = !animating;


    document.getElementById('play').textContent = animating ? 'Pause' : 'Play';


    if (animating) loop();


  });





  document.getElementById('clear').addEventListener('click', () => {


    clearBalls();


    draw();


  });





  // Canvas click adds a ball


  canvas.addEventListener('click', (e) => {


    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();


    const scaleX = W / rect.width;


    const scaleY = H / rect.height;


    const x = (e.clientX - rect.left) * scaleX;


    const y = (e.clientY - rect.top) * scaleY;


    addBall(x, y, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4, 0, 10 + Math.random() * 8);


    if (!animating) draw();


  });





  // Initial draw


  draw();


})();


</script>


</body>


</html>

gpt-oss-20b — 1.000 · 8/8 tests passed (rescored) · 5.7s · 1,091 tok out · 227 think 
```html


<!DOCTYPE html>


<html lang="en">


<head>


<meta charset="UTF-8">


<title>Bouncing Ball Playground</title>


<style>


  body{


    background:#222;


    color:#eee;


    font-family:Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif;


    text-align:center;


    margin-top:20px;


  }


  canvas{border:1px solid #555; display:block; margin:auto;}


  button{margin:5px; padding:8px 12px; background:#444; color:#eee; border:none; cursor:pointer;}


  button:hover{background:#666;}


</style>


</head>


<body>





<canvas id="sim" width="400" height="400"></canvas>


<div>


  <button id="drop">Drop Ball</button>


  <button id="play">Play</button>


  <button id="clear">Clear</button>


</div>





<script>


(() => {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const w = canvas.width, h = canvas.height;


  const gravity = 0.5;          // pixels per step squared


  const restitution = 0.9;      // energy loss on bounce





  let balls = [];


  let playing = false;


  let rafId;





  function draw() {


    ctx.fillStyle='#222';


    ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);


    for (const b of balls) {


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.arc(b.x,b.y,b.r,0,Math.PI*2);


      ctx.fillStyle = '#88f';


      ctx.fill();


      ctx.strokeStyle = '#55c';


      ctx.stroke();


    }


  }





  function step(dt=1){


    for (const b of balls) {


      // gravity


      b.vy += gravity * dt;


      // motion


      b.x += b.vx * dt;


      b.y += b.vy * dt;





      // collision with walls


      if (b.x - b.r < 0) { b.x = b.r; b.vx *= -restitution; }


      else if (b.x + b.r > w) { b.x = w - b.r; b.vx *= -restitution; }





      if (b.y - b.r < 0) { b.y = b.r; b.vy *= -restitution; }


      else if (b.y + b.r > h) { b.y = h - b.r; b.vy *= -restitution; }


    }


    draw();


  }





  function addBall(x,y,vx=0,vy=0,r){


    r = typeof r === 'number' ? r : Math.random()*10+10;


    balls.push({x,y,vx,vy,r});


    draw();


  }





  // API


  window.sim = {


    w,


    h,


    balls: () => balls.map(b=>({...b})),


    addBall,


    clear: () => {balls=[]; draw();},


    step


  };





  // UI handlers


  document.getElementById('drop').addEventListener('click', ()=>addBall(w/2,h/2));


  document.getElementById('clear').addEventListener('click', ()=>window.sim.clear());





  canvas.addEventListener('click',(e)=>{


    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();


    const x = e.clientX - rect.left;


    const y = e.clientY - rect.top;


    addBall(x,y);


  });





  document.getElementById('play').addEventListener('click',()=>{


    playing = !playing;


    if (playing){


      document.getElementById('play').textContent='Pause';


      function loop(){step(); rafId=requestAnimationFrame(loop);}


      rafId = requestAnimationFrame(loop);


    } else {


      document.getElementById('play').textContent='Play';


      cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);


    }


  });





  // initial draw


  draw();


})();


</script>


</body>


</html>

laguna-xs-2.1 — 1.000 · 8/8 tests passed (rescored) · 9.2s · 1,815 tok out · 243 think 
```html


<!DOCTYPE html>


<html lang="en">


<head>


<meta charset="UTF-8">


<title>Bouncing Ball Physics Playground</title>


<style>


* { margin: 0; padding: 0; box-sizing: border-box; }


body {


  background: #1a1a2e;


  color: #eee;


  font-family: Arial, sans-serif;


  display: flex;


  flex-direction: column;


  align-items: center;


  min-height: 100vh;


  padding: 20px;


}


#sim {


  background: #16213e;


  border: 2px solid #0f3460;


  cursor: crosshair;


  margin-bottom: 15px;


}


button {


  background: #0f3460;


  color: #fff;


  border: 2px solid #e94560;


  padding: 10px 20px;


  margin: 0 5px;


  font-size: 16px;


  cursor: pointer;


  transition: all 0.2s;


}


button:hover { background: #e94560; }


button:active { transform: scale(0.95); }


</style>


</head>


<body>


<canvas id="sim" width="500" height="500"></canvas>


<div>


  <button id="drop">Drop</button>


  <button id="play">Play</button>


  <button id="clear">Clear</button>


</div>





<script>


(() => {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  


  let balls = [];


  let animationId = null;


  let running = false;


  


  // Physics constants


  const GRAVITY = 0.5;


  const RESTITUTION = 0.8;


  const FRICTION = 0.99;


  


  // Get canvas dimensions


  function getCanvasSize() {


    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();


    return { w: rect.width, h: rect.height };


  }


  


  // Add a ball with optional parameters


  function addBall(x, y, vx = 0, vy = 0, r = 20) {


    balls.push({ x, y, vx, vy, r });


  }


  


  // Get all balls (for API)


  function getBalls() {


    return balls.map(b => ({ ...b }));


  }


  


  // Clear all balls


  function clear() {


    balls = [];


  }


  


  // Step the simulation


  function step(dt = 1) {


    const { w, h } = getCanvasSize();


    


    for (let ball of balls) {


      // Apply gravity


      ball.vy += GRAVITY * dt;


      


      // Update position


      ball.x += ball.vx * dt;


      ball.y += ball.vy * dt;


      


      // Bounce off left wall


      if (ball.x - ball.r < 0) {


        ball.x = ball.r;


        ball.vx = -ball.vx * RESTITUTION;


        ball.vx *= FRICTION;


      }


      


      // Bounce off right wall


      if (ball.x + ball.r > w) {


        ball.x = w - ball.r;


        ball.vx = -ball.vx * RESTITUTION;


        ball.vx *= FRICTION;


      }


      


      // Bounce off top wall


      if (ball.y - ball.r < 0) {


        ball.y = ball.r;


        ball.vy = -ball.vy * RESTITUTION;


        ball.vy *= FRICTION;


      }


      


      // Bounce off bottom wall


      if (ball.y + ball.r > h) {


        ball.y = h - ball.r;


        ball.vy = -ball.vy * RESTITUTION;


        ball.vy *= FRICTION;


      }


    }


    


    draw();


  }


  


  // Draw everything


  function draw() {


    const { w, h } = getCanvasSize();


    


    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);


    


    for (let ball of balls) {


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, ball.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


      ctx.fillStyle = '#e94560';


      ctx.fill();


      ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff';


      ctx.lineWidth = 2;


      ctx.stroke();


    }


  }


  


  // Animation loop


  function animate() {


    step(1);


    animationId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);


  }


  


  // Start/pause simulation


  function togglePlay() {


    running = !running;


    if (running) {


      play.textContent = 'Pause';


      animate();


    } else {


      play.textContent = 'Play';


      if (animationId) {


        cancelAnimationFrame(animationId);


      }


    }


  }


  


  // Handle canvas click to add ball


  canvas.addEventListener('click', (e) => {


    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();


    const x = e.clientX - rect.left;


    const y = e.clientY - rect.top;


    addBall(x, y);


    if (!running) draw();


  });


  


  // Button event listeners


  document.getElementById('drop').addEventListener('click', () => {


    const { w, h } = getCanvasSize();


    addBall(w / 2, 50);


    if (!running) draw();


  });


  


  document.getElementById('play').addEventListener('click', togglePlay);


  


  document.getElementById('clear').addEventListener('click', () => {


    clear();


    running = false;


    play.textContent = 'Play';


    if (animationId) {


      cancelAnimationFrame(animationId);


    }


    draw();


  });


  


  // Initial draw


  draw();


  


  // Expose API on window.sim


  const size = getCanvasSize();


  window.sim = {


    w: size.w,


    h: size.h,


    balls: () => getBalls(),


    addBall: (x, y, vx = 0, vy = 0, r) => addBall(x, y, vx, vy, r || 20),


    clear: () => { clear(); draw(); },


    step: (dt = 1) => step(dt)


  };


  


})();


</script>


</body>


</html>

```html


<!DOCTYPE html>


<html lang="en">

<script>


(function () {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


  const ctx    = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const btnDrop = document.getElementById('drop');


  const btnPlay = document.getElementById('play');


  const btnClear = document.getElementById('clear');





  /* ---- state ---- */


  let running = false;


  let animId  = null;





  const GRAVITY      = 600;   // px/s²


  const RESTITUTION  = 0.78;


  const BASE_DT      = 1 / 60; // one frame at 60 fps


  const balls        = [];





  /* ---- helpers ---- */


  function randomColor () {


    const palette = [


      '#e94560','#f3a683','#ffb8b8','#ff6f61','#ffcc5c',


      '#ffd166','#06d6a0','#118ab2','#9b5de5','#f15bb5',


      '#7209b7','#4cc9f0','#4361ee','#4895ef'


    ];


    return palette[Math.floor(Math.random() * palette.length)];


  }





  function clampBall (b) {


    b.x = Math.max(b.r, Math.min(canvas.width - b.r, b.x));


    b.y = Math.max(b.r, Math.min(canvas.height - b.r, b.y));


  }





  /* ---- drawing ---- */


  function clearCanvas () {


    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);


  }





  function drawBall (b) {


    // Shadow


    ctx.beginPath();


    ctx.arc(b.x + 2, b.y + 2, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.25)';


    ctx.fill();





    // Main circle


    ctx.beginPath();


    ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


    ctx.fillStyle = b.color;


    ctx.fill();





    // Specular highlight


    ctx.beginPath();


    const hs = b.r * 0.45;


    ctx.arc(b.x - b.r * 0.32, b.y - b.r * 0.32, hs, 0, Math.PI * 2);


    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.32)';


    ctx.fill();





    // Thin rim


    ctx.beginPath();


    ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)';


    ctx.lineWidth = 1;


    ctx.stroke();


  }





  function render () {


    clearCanvas();


    for (let i = balls.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) drawBall(balls[i]);


  }





  /* ---- physics step ---- */


  function step (dt) {


    if (dt == null || dt === undefined) dt = 1;


    const frameDt = BASE_DT * dt;


    const gravStep = GRAVITY * frameDt;





    for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {


      const b = balls[i];





      // Gravity


      b.vy += gravStep;





      // Integrate


      b.x += b.vx * frameDt;


      b.y += b.vy * frameDt;





      // --- floor ---


      if (b.y + b.r > canvas.height) {


        b.y = canvas.height - b.r;


        b.vy = -Math.abs(b.vy) * RESTITUTION;


        b.vx *= 0.995;            // slight friction on contact


        if (Math.abs(b.vy) < 6) b.vy = 0;


      }





      // --- ceiling ---


      if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


        b.y = b.r;


        b.vy = Math.abs(b.vy) * RESTITUTION;


      }





      // --- right wall ---


      if (b.x + b.r > canvas.width) {


        b.x = canvas.width - b.r;


        b.vx = -Math.abs(b.vx) * RESTITUTION;


      }





      // --- left wall ---


      if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


        b.x = b.r;


        b.vx = Math.abs(b.vx) * RESTITUTION;


      }


    }





    render();


  }





  /* ---- public API helpers ---- */


  function addBall (x, y, vx, vy, r) {


    const radius = (r != null && typeof r === 'number') ? r : (15 + Math.random() * 20);


    const ball = {


      x:   x != null ? x : canvas.width / 2,


      y:   y != null ? y : canvas.height / 2,


      vx:  vx != null ? Number(vx) : 0,


      vy:  v

<script>


    /**


     * Physics Playground Logic


     */


    (function() {


        const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


        const dropBtn = document.getElementById('drop');


        const playBtn = document.getElementById('play');


        const clearBtn = document.getElementById('clear');





        const gravity = 0.25;


        const restitution = 0.8; // Energy loss on bounce


        let isPlaying = true;


        let balls = [];





        class Ball {


            constructor(x, y, vx, vy, r) {


                this.x = x;


                this.y = y;


                this.vx = vx;


                this.vy = vy;


                this.r = r;


                this.color = `hsl(${Math.random() * 360}, 70%, 60%)`;


            }





            draw(ctx) {


                ctx.beginPath();


                ctx.arc(this.x, this.y, this.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


                ctx.fillStyle = this.color;


                ctx.fill();


                ctx.closePath();


            }





            update(dt, w, h) {


                // Apply gravity


                this.vy += gravity * dt;


                


                // Apply velocity


                this.x += this.vx * dt;


                this.y += this.vy * dt;





                // Collision detection: Floor/Ceiling


                if (this.y - this.r < 0) {


                    this.y = this.r;


                    this.vy = -this.vy * restitution;


                } else if (this.y + this.r > h) {


                    this.y = h - this.r;


                    this.vy = -this.vy * restitution;


                }





                // Collision detection: Walls


                if (this.x - this.r < 0) {


                    this.x = this.r;


                    this.vx = -this.vx * restitution;


                } else if (this.x + this.r > w) {


                    this.x = w - this.r;


                    this.vx = -this.vx * restitution;


                }


            }


        }





        function render() {


            if (isPlaying) {


                sim.step(1);


            }


            requestAnimationFrame(render);


        }





        // The Programmatic API


        window.sim = {


            get w() { return canvas.width; },


            get h() { return canvas.height; },


            balls: () => [...balls],


            addBall: (x, y, vx = 0, vy = 0, r) => {


                const radius = r || 15;


                balls.push(new Ball(x, y, vx, vy, radius));


                draw();


            },


            clear: () => {


                balls = [];


                draw();


            },


            step: (dt = 1) => {


                for (let ball of balls) {


                    ball.update(dt, canvas.width, canvas.height);


                }


                draw();


            }


        };





        function draw() {


            ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);


            for (let ball of balls) {


                ball.draw(ctx);


            }


        }





        // Event Listeners


        canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {


            const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();


            const x = e.clientX - rect.left;


            const y = e.clientY - rect.top;


            window.sim.addBall(x, y, Math.random() * 10 - 5, Math.random

    <script>


        // --- CONSTANTS AND INITIALIZATION ---


        const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');





        const CANVAS_W = 800; // Internal drawing resolution width (Must be >= 400)


        const CANVAS_H = 600; // Internal drawing resolution height (Must be >= 400)





        canvas.width = CANVAS_W;


        canvas.height = CANVAS_H;





        // Simulation parameters


        let balls = [];


        const GRAVITY = 9.8 / 100; // Scaled gravity for smoother simulation steps


        const RESTITUTION = 0.95;  // Energy loss on bounce


        const BALL_COLOR = '#fab38b';





        let isRunning = false;


        let animationFrameId = null;


        const TIME_STEP = 1 / 60; // Target simulation delta time (dt)





        // --- UTILITY FUNCTIONS ---





        /**


         * Draws the current state of all balls on the canvas.


         */


        function draw() {


            ctx.clearRect(0, 0, CANVAS_W, CANVAS_H);





            balls.forEach(ball => {


                ctx.beginPath();


                ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, ball.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


                ctx.fillStyle = BALL_COLOR;


                ctx.fill();


                ctx.closePath();


            });


        }





        /**


         * Updates the physics for one time step (dt). Handles movement, gravity, and collisions.


         * @param {number} dt - Time delta step.


         */


        function updatePhysics(dt) {


            for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {


                const ball = balls[i];





                // 1. Apply Gravity


                ball.vy += GRAVITY * dt;





                // 2. Update Position


                ball.x += ball.vx * dt;


                ball.y += ball.vy * dt;





                // 3. Collision Detection & Response (Edges)





                // Bounce off Left/Right Walls (X-axis boundaries)


                if (ball.x - ball.r < 0) {


                    ball.x = ball.r; // Clamp position


                    ball.vx *= -RESTITUTION; // Reverse and lose energy


                } else if (ball.x + ball.r > CANVAS_W) {


                    ball.x = CANVAS_W - ball.r; // Clamp position


                    ball.vx *= -RESTITUTION;


                }





                // Bounce off Top/Bottom Walls (Y-axis boundaries)


                if (ball.y - ball.r < 0) {


                    ball.y = ball.r; // Clamp position


                    ball.vy *= -RESTITUTION;


                } else if (ball.y + ball.r > CANVAS_H) {


                    ball.y = CANVAS_H - ball.r; // Clamp position


                    ball.vy *= -RESTITUTION;


                    // Optional: Add a small friction effect when hitting the floor/ceiling


                    // This helps stabilize things if GRAVITY was too high


                }





                // 4. Cleanup (Remove balls that fall too far away or have minimal energy)

(function() {


    // --- Configuration & Constants ---


    const GRAVITY = 0.4;       // Downward acceleration per step


    const FRICTION_X = 1.0;    // No air resistance on walls (bouncy)


    const RESTITUTION = 0.85;  // Energy loss on bounce (< 1 = energy loss)


    


    // --- State ---


    const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


    const width = canvas.width;


    const height = canvas.height;





    // Store balls in a flat array. We will attach this to the API object for easy access


    let ballsArray = [];


    


    // Animation state


    let isPlaying = true;


    let animationFrameId = null;





    // --- Helper Functions ---


    


    function random(min, max) {


        return Math.random() * (max - min) + min;


    }





    function randomColor() {


        const hue = Math.floor(Math.random() * 360);


        return `hsl(${hue}, 80%, 60%)`;


    }





    // --- Physics Engine (step) ---


    


    function step(dt = 1) {


        // Update physics for all balls


        ballsArray.forEach(ball => {


            // Apply Gravity


            ball.vy += GRAVITY;





            // Update Position


            ball.x += ball.vx * dt;


            ball.y += ball.vy * dt;





            // --- Collision Detection & Response (Walls) ---





            // Floor (Bottom)


            if (ball.y + ball.r > height) {


                ball.y = height - ball.r; // Clamp position


                ball.vy *= -RESTITUTION;  // Reverse velocity with loss


            } 


            // Ceiling (Top)


            else if (ball.y - ball.r < 0) {


                ball.y = ball.r;


                ball.vy *= -RESTITUTION;


            }





            // Right Wall


            if (ball.x + ball.r > width) {


                ball.x = width - ball.r;


                ball.vx *= -RESTITUTION;


            } 


            // Left Wall


            else if (ball.x - ball.r < 0) {


                ball.x = ball.r;


                ball.vx *= -RESTITUTION;


            }





            // Visual polish: slightly increase mass or radius over time? No, keep constant.


        });





        draw();





        if (isPlaying) {


            animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(() => step());


        }


    }

        // Clear canvas


        ctx.fillStyle = '#111'; // Dark background for the canvas content


        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);

<script>


(function() {


  // ─── Canvas Setup ────────────────────────────────────────


  const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const W = canvas.width;


  const H = canvas.height;





  window.sim = { w: W, h: H };





  // ─── Physics Constants ────────────────────────────────────


  const GRAVITY = 0.5;


  const FRICTION = 1 - 0.08;       // air resistance per step


  const BOUNCE = 0.75;             // restitution (energy loss on bounce)





  // ─── Ball State ────────────────────────────────────────────


  let balls = [];


  let isPlaying = true;





  function createBall(x, y, vx, vy, r) {


    return { x: x + Math.random() * 10 - 5, y: y + Math.random() * 10 - 5, vx: vx || (Math.random() > 0.5 ? 2 : -2), vy: vy || 0, r: r || 8 };


  }





  // ─── Physics Engine ────────────────────────────────────────


  function step(dt) {


    const subSteps = dt; // for stability





    for (let s = 0; s < subSteps; s++) {


      balls.forEach(ball => {


        ball.vy += GRAVITY * dt;


        ball.vx *= FRICTION;


        ball.vy *= FRICTION;





        ball.x += ball.vx * dt;


        ball.y += ball.vy * dt;





        // Floor collision


        if (ball.y + ball.r > H) {


          ball.y = H - ball.r;


          ball.vy = -ball.vy * BOUNCE;


        }


        // Ceiling collision


        else if (ball.y - ball.r < 0) {


          ball.y = ball.r;


          ball.vy = -ball.vy * BOUNCE;


        }





        // Right wall collision


        if (ball.x + ball.r > W) {


          ball.x = W - ball.r;


          ball.vx = -ball.vx * BOUNCE;


        }


        // Left wall collision


        else if (ball.x - ball.r < 0) {


          ball.x = ball.r;


          ball.vx = -ball.vx * BOUNCE;


        }





        // Prevent tunneling: clamp to bounds


        ball.x = Math.max(ball.r, Math.min(W - ball.r, ball.x));


        ball.y = Math.max(ball.r, Math.min(H - ball.r, ball.y));


      });


    }


  }





  // ─── Rendering ──────────────────────────────────────────────


  function draw() {


    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);





    balls.forEach(ball => {


      const gradient = ctx.createRadialGradient(


        ball.x - ball.r * 0.3, ball.y - ball.r * 0.3, ball.r * 0.1,


        ball.x, ball.y, ball.r


      );





      // Color based on ball index for variety


      const hue = (ball._hue || 200) % 360;


      gradient.addColorStop(0, `hsl(${hue}, 85%, 70%)`);


      gradient.addColorStop(1, `hsl(${hue}, 85%, 40%)`);





      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, ball.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


      ctx.fillStyle = gradient;


      ctx.fill();





      // White highlight for "glossy" look


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.arc(


        ball.x - ball.r * 0.3, ball.y - ball.r * 0.3,


        ball.r * 0.25, 0, Math.PI * 2


      );


      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.4)';


      ctx.fill();





      // Shadow on ground


      const shadowGrad = ctx.createLinearGradient(ball.x, ball.y + ball.r, ball.x, ball.y + ball.r + 10);


      shadowGrad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)');


      shadowGrad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');


      ctx.beginPath();

<script>


(function () {


  'use strict';





  var SIZE = 500;            // logical pixels (>= 400)


  var GRAVITY = 0.5;         // downward acceleration per step at dt = 1


  var RESTITUTION = 0.8;     // fraction of speed retained on a bounce (< 1)


  var REST_THRESHOLD = 1.0;  // below this speed near an edge, the ball rests





  var canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


  var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  var dpr = Math.max(1, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);





  // Render at device resolution but keep a logical coordinate space of SIZE x SIZE.


  canvas.width = SIZE * dpr;


  canvas.height = SIZE * dpr;


  ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);





  var balls = []; // internal: { x, y, vx, vy, r, hue }





  function clamp(v, lo, hi) { return v < lo ? lo : (v > hi ? hi : v); }


  function randHue() { return Math.floor(Math.random() * 360); }





  // Internal adder (pure: only mutates state).


  function addBall(x, y, vx, vy, r) {


    if (vx == null) vx = 0;


    if (vy == null) vy = 0;


    if (r == null || isNaN(r)) r = 12 + Math.random() * 14; // 12..26 px


    balls.push({


      x: clamp(x, r, SIZE - r),


      y: clamp(y, r, SIZE - r),


      vx: vx,


      vy: vy,


      r: r,


      hue: randHue()


    });


    return balls[balls.length - 1];


  }





  function step(dt) {


    if (dt == null) dt = 1;


    var g = GRAVITY * dt;


    for (var i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {


      var b = balls[i];





      // Gravity accelerates the ball downward (+y in canvas space).


      b.vy += g;





      // Integrate position.


      b.x += b.vx * dt;


      b.y += b.vy * dt;





      // Resolve vertical collisions: clamp inside, reverse + damp perpendicular velocity.


      if (b.y + b.r > SIZE) {


        b.y = SIZE - b.r;


        if (Math.abs(b.vy) < REST_THRESHOLD) b.vy = 0;


        else b.vy = -Math.abs(b.vy) * RESTITUTION;


      } else if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


        b.y = b.r;


        if (Math.abs(b.vy) < REST_THRESHOLD) b.vy = 0;


        else b.vy = Math.abs(b.vy) * RESTITUTION;


      }





      // Resolve horizontal collisions the same way.


      if (b.x + b.r > SIZE) {


        b.x = SIZE - b.r;


        if (Math.abs(b.vx) < REST_THRESHOLD) b.vx = 0;


        else b.vx = -Math.abs(b.vx) * RESTITUTION;


      } else if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


        b.x = b.r;


        if (Math.abs(b.vx) < REST_THRESHOLD) b.vx = 0;


        else b.vx = Math.abs(b.vx) * RESTI

<script>


(() => {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const W = canvas.width, H = canvas.height;





  const GRAVITY = 0.5;


  const RESTITUTION = 0.8;





  const balls = [];


  let running = false;


  let lastTime = 0;





  function draw() {


    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);


    for (const b of balls) {


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


      ctx.fillStyle = `hsl(${b.hue}, 80%, 60%)`;


      ctx.fill();


      ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff3';


      ctx.lineWidth = 1;


      ctx.stroke();


    }


  }





  function update(dt) {


    for (const b of balls) {


      b.vy += GRAVITY * dt;


      b.x += b.vx * dt;


      b.y += b.vy * dt;





      if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


        b.x = b.r;


        b.vx *= -RESTITUTION;


      } else if (b.x + b.r > W) {


        b.x = W - b.r;


        b.vx *= -RESTITUTION;


      }


      if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


        b.y = b.r;


        b.vy *= -RESTITUTION;


      } else if (b.y + b.r > H) {


        b.y = H - b.r;


        b.vy *= -RESTITUTION;


      }


    }


  }





  function step(dt = 1) {


    update(dt);


    draw();


  }





  function addBall(x, y, vx = 0, vy = 0, r = 20) {


    balls.push({


      x, y, vx, vy, r,


      hue: Math.random() * 360


    });


  }





  function clear() {


    balls.length = 0;


    draw();


  }





  function loop(time) {


    if (running) {


      const dt = (time - lastTime) / 16.67; // normalize to ~60fps steps


      lastTime = time;


      step(dt);


      requestAnimationFrame(loop);


    }


  }





  // Event listeners


  document.getElementById('drop').addEventListener('click', () => {


    addBall(W / 2, 50, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4, 0, 15 + Math.random() * 20);


  });





  const playBtn = document.getElementById('play');


  playBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {


    running = !running;


    playBtn.classList.toggle('playing', running);


    playBtn.textContent = running ? 'Pause' : 'Play';


    if (running) {


      lastTime = performance.now();


      requestAnimationFrame(loop);


    }


  });





  document.getElementById('clear').addEventListener('click', clear);





  canvas.addEventListener('click', e => {


    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();


    const x = e.clientX - rect.left;


    const y = e.clientY - rect.top;


    addBall(x, y, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 6, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 6, 10 + Math.random() * 25);


  });





  // Expose API


  window.sim = {


    get w() { return W; },


    get h() { return H; },


    balls: () => balls.map(b => ({ x: b.x, y: b.y, vx: b.vx, vy: b.vy, r: b.r })),


    addBall,


    clear,


    step


  };





  // Initial draw


  draw();


})();


</script>


</body>


</html>

<script>


const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


const w = canvas.width;


const h = canvas.height;





let balls = [];


const gravity = 0.5;


const restitution = 0.85;





function draw(){


  ctx.fillStyle = '#0a0a0a';


  ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);


  for(const b of balls){


    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(b.x - b.r*0.3, b.y - b.r*0.3, b.r*0.1, b.x, b.y, b.r);


    g.addColorStop(0,'#ffffff');


    g.addColorStop(1,'#3a8dde');


    ctx.beginPath();


    ctx.arc(b.x,b.y,b.r,0,Math.PI*2);


    ctx.fillStyle = g;


    ctx.fill();


    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)';


    ctx.stroke();


  }


}





function step(dt=1){


  for(const b of balls){


    b.vy += gravity * dt;


    b.x += b.vx * dt;


    b.y += b.vy * dt;





    if(b.x - b.r < 0){ b.x = b.r; b.vx = -b.vx * restitution; }


    else if(b.x + b.r > w){ b.x = w - b.r; b.vx = -b.vx * restitution; }





    if(b.y - b.r < 0){ b.y = b.r; b.vy = -b.vy * restitution; }


    else if(b.y + b.r > h){ b.y = h - b.r; b.vy = -b.vy * restitution; }


  }


  draw();


}





window.sim = {


  w,


  h,


  balls(){ return balls.map(b=>({x:b.x,y:b.y,vx:b.vx,vy:b.vy,r:b.r})); },


  addBall(x,y,vx=0,vy=0,r){


    if(r==null) r = 15 + Math.random()*15;


    balls.push({x,y,vx,vy,r});


    draw();


  },


  clear(){ balls = []; draw(); },


  step


};





document.getElementById('drop').onclick = () => {


  const r = 10 + Math.random()*20;


  const x = r + Math.random()*(w-2*r);


  const y = r + 20;


  window.sim.addBall(x,y,(Math.random()-0.5)*4,0,r);


};





document.getElementById('clear').onclick = () => window.sim.clear();





let playing = false, animId=null;


document.getElementById('play').onclick = (e)=>{


  playing = !playing;


  e.target.textContent = playing ? 'Pause' : 'Play';


  if(playing){


    const loop = ()=>{ window.sim.step(1); animId = requestAnimationFrame(loop); };


    loop();


  }else cancelAnimationFrame(animId);


};





canvas.addEventListener('click', e=>{


  const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();


  const sx = canvas.width / rect.width;


  const sy = canvas.height / rect.height;


  const x = (e.clientX - rect.left)*sx;


  const y = (e.clientY - rect.top)*sy;


  window.sim.addBall(x,y,0,0);


});





draw();


</script>


</body>


</html>

        const restitution = 0.8;





        let animationId = null;


        let lastTime = 0;


        let isRunning = false;





        function draw() {


            ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);


            for (const b of balls) {


                ctx.beginPath();


                ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


                ctx.fillStyle = '#0f0';


                ctx.fill();


            }


        }





        function animate(currentTime) {


            if (!isRunning) return;


            const dt = (currentTime - lastTime) / 1000; // seconds


            window.sim.step(dt);


            lastTime = currentTime;


            animationId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);


        }





        canvas.addEventListener('click', (e) => {


            const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();


            const x = e.clientX - rect.left;


            const y = e.clientY - rect.top;


            window.sim.addBall(x, y);


        });





        document.getElementById('drop').addEventListener('click', () => {


            window.sim.addBall(canvas.width / 2, 50, 0, 0, 20);


        });





        document.getElementById('clear').addEventListener('click', () => {


            window.sim.clear();


        });





        document.getElementById('play').addEventListener('click', () => {


            if (isRunning) {


                cancelAnimationFrame(animationId);


                isRunning = false;


                document.getElementById('play').textContent = 'Play';


            } else {


                lastTime = performance.now();


                animate(lastTime);


                isRunning = true;


                document.getElementById('play').textContent = 'Pause';


            }


        });





        window.sim = {


            w: canvas.width,


            h: canvas.height,


            balls() {


                return balls.map(b => ({ ...b }));


            },


            addBall(x, y, vx=0, vy=0, r=20) {


                const radius = r || 20;


                balls.push({ x, y, vx, vy, r: radius });


            },


            clear() {


                balls = [];


            },


            step(dt) {


                for (let b of balls) {


                    // Apply gravity


                    b.vy += gravity * dt;





                    // Update position and handle collisions with walls


                    // X-axis


                    b.x += b.vx * dt;


                    if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


                        b.x = b.r;


                        b.vx *= -restitution;


                    } else if (b.x + b.r > this.w) {


                        b.x = this.w - b.r;


                        b.vx *= -restitution;


                    }





                    // Y-axis


                    b.y += b.vy * dt;


                    if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


                        b.y = b.r;


                        b.vy *= -restitution;


                    } else if (b.y + b.r > this.h) {


                        b.y = this.h - b.r;


                        b.vy *= -restitution;


                    }


                }





                draw();


            },


        };


    </script>


</body>


</html>

            step(dt = 1) {


                // Update Physics


                for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {

                        b.vy *= -RESTITUTION;

                        b.vx *= -RESTITUTION;


                    } else if (b.x - b.r < 0) { // Left Wall


                        b.x = b.r;


                        b.vx *= -RESTITUTION;


                    }


                }


                draw();


            }


        };

                ctx.fill();


                ctx.closePath();


            }


        }

        function loop() {


            if (isPlaying) {


                window.sim.step();


            }


            requestAnimationFrame(loop);


        }

<!DOCTYPE html>


<html lang="en">


<head>

<title>Bouncing‑Ball Playground</title>


<style>

    }

        background:#111;

        margin:auto;


        border:2px solid #555;


    }


    button{


        background:#444;


        border:none;


        color:#fff;

        border-radius:4px;


        font-size:14px;

<div class="wrapper">


    <canvas id="sim" width="600" height="600"></canvas><br>


    <button id="drop">Drop Ball</button>


    <button id="play">Pause</button>


    <button id="clear">Clear</button>


</div>





<script>


(() => {


    const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');





    const W = canvas.width;


    const H = canvas.height;





    const GRAVITY = 0.35;      // px per step²


    const RESTITUTION = 0.78;   // bounce energy retention


    const MAX_SPEED = 20;       // safety cap for tunnelling





    let balls = [];


    let animating = true;


    let lastTime = null;





    // ---------- Rendering ----------


    function draw() {


        ctx.fillStyle = '#111';


        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);


        ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffdd33';


        ctx.lineWidth = 2;


        for (const b of balls) {


            ctx.beginPath();


            ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, Math.PI*2);


            ctx.stroke();


        }


    }





    // ---------- Physics ----------


    function step(dt = 1) {


        for (let b of balls) {


            // apply gravity


            b.vy += GRAVITY * dt;





            // cap speeds to avoid tunnelling


            if (b.vx > MAX_SPEED) b.vx = MAX_SPEED;


            if (b.vx < -MAX_SPEED) b.vx = -MAX_SPEED;


            if (b.vy > MAX_SPEED) b.vy = MAX_SPEED;


            if (b.vy < -MAX_SPEED) b.vy = -MAX_SPEED;





            // integrate


            b.x += b.vx * dt;


            b.y += b.vy * dt;





            // wall collisions


            if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


                b.x = b.r;


                b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


            } else if (b.x + b.r > W) {


                b.x = W - b.r;


                b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


            }





            if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


                b.y = b.r;


                b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


            } else if (b.y + b.r > H) {


                b.y = H - b.r;


                b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


                // small floor friction to eventually stop jitter


                b.vx *= 0.99;


            }


        }


        draw();


    }





    // ---------- API ----------


    window.sim = {


        get w(){ return W; },


        get h(){ return H; },


        balls(){


            // shallow copy of state objects


            return balls.map(b=>({x:b.x, y:b.y, vx:b.vx, vy:b.vy, r:b.r}));


        },


        addBall(x, y, vx = 0, vy = 0, r = 15){


            // keep inside bounds


            const rad = Math.max(5, r);


            const bx = Math.min(Math.max(rad, x), W - rad);


            const by = Math.min(Math.max(rad, y), H - rad);


            balls.push({x:bx, y:by, vx, vy, r:rad});


            draw();


        },


        clear(){


            balls = [];


            draw();


        },


        step(dt = 1){


            step(dt);


        }


    };





    // ---------- UI ----------


    document.getElementById('drop').addEventListener('click', () => {


        const x = Math.random()* (W-40) + 20;


        const y = 30;


        sim.addBall(x, y, (Math.random()-0.5)*6, 0, 12+Math.random()*8);


    });





    const playBtn = document.getElementById('play');


    playBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {


        animating = !animating;


        playBtn.textContent = animating ? 'Pause' : 'Play';


        if (animating) requestAnimationFrame(loop);


    });





    document.getElementById('clear').addEventListener('click', () => sim.clear());





    canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', ev => {


        const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();


        const x = ev.clientX - rect.left;


        const y = ev.clientY - rect.top;


        sim.addBall(x, y, (Math.random()-0.5)*4, -5, 14);


    });





    // ---------- Animation Loop ----------


    function loop(timestamp){


        if (!lastTime) lastTime = timestamp;


        const delta = (timestamp - lastTime) / 16.666; // approx 60fps steps


        lastTime = timestamp;


        if (animating) {


            step(delta);


            requestAnimationFrame(loop);


        }


    }


    // start


    draw();


    requestAnimationFrame(loop);


})();


</script>


</body>


</html>

<script>


(() => {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const width = canvas.width;


  const height = canvas.height;





  const GRAVITY = 0.35;


  const RESTITUTION = 0.82;


  const FRICTION = 0.995;





  let balls = [];


  let playing = false;


  let animationId = null;





  function randomR() {


    return Math.max(8, Math.floor(Math.random() * 22));


  }





  function randomColor() {


    const h = Math.floor(Math.random() * 360);


    return `hsl(${h}, 75%, 55%)`;


  }





  function randV() {


    return (Math.random() - 0.5) * 6;


  }





  function addBall(x, y, vx = 0, vy = 0, r = randomR()) {


    // Clamp inside the canvas so it doesn't start out of bounds


    r = Math.max(3, r);


    x = Math.max(r, Math.min(width - r, x));


    y = Math.max(r, Math.min(height - r, y));


    balls.push({ x, y, vx, vy, r, color: randomColor() });


  }





  function step(dt = 1) {


    // Advance physics for each ball


    for (const b of balls) {


      b.vx *= FRICTION;


      b.vy *= FRICTION;


      b.vy += GRAVITY * dt;





      b.x += b.vx * dt;


      b.y += b.vy * dt;





      // Bounce off walls


      if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


        b.x = b.r;


        b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


      } else if (b.x + b.r > width) {


        b.x = width - b.r;


        b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


      }





      // Bounce off floor / ceiling


      if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


        b.y = b.r;


        b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


      } else if (b.y + b.r > height) {


        b.y = height - b.r;


        b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


      }


    }


    draw();


  }





  function draw() {


    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);





    // Grid-ish background dots for reference


    ctx.fillStyle = '#1e1e30';


    for (let i = 0; i <= width; i += 40) {


      for (let j = 0; j <= height; j += 40) {


        ctx.beginPath();


        ctx.arc(i, j, 1.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);


        ctx.fill();


      }


    }





    // Draw balls


    for (const b of balls) {


      const gradient = ctx.createRadialGradient(


        b.x - b.r / 3, b.y - b.r / 3, 0.2,


        b.x, b.y, b.r


      );


      gradient.addColorStop(0, b.color);


      gradient.addColorStop(1, '#000');





      ctx.fillStyle = gradient;


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


      ctx.fill();





      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.4)';


      ctx.lineWidth = 1;


      ctx.stroke();


    }


  }





  function clear() {


    balls = [];


    draw();


  }





  // Animation loop


  function animate() {


    step();


    animationId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);


  }





  // Button handlers


  document.getElementById('drop').addEventListener('click', () => {


    addBall(width / 2, 20, randV(), 0);


  });





  document.getElementById('play').addEventListener('click', () => {


    playing = !playing;


    const btn = document.getElementById('play');


    btn.textContent = playing ? 'Pause' : 'Play';


    if (playing) {


      animate();


    } else {


      cancelAnimationFrame(animationId);


    }


  });





  document.getElementById('clear').addEventListener('click', () => clear());

<script>


(function () {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');





  // Logical / rendering size — use CSS pixels for size, but back the canvas


  // with a matching internal resolution.


  const W = canvas.width;


  const H = canvas.height;





  // Simulation state


  const balls = [];


  const GRAVITY = 0.5;


  const RESTITUTION = 0.85;


  const AIR_FRICTION = 0.999;


  const MAX_SUBSTEPS = 8;





  // Animation


  let playing = true;


  let lastTime = performance.now();


  let rafId = null;





  // ---------- API ----------


  window.sim = {


    w: W,


    h: H,


    balls() {


      return balls.map(b => ({


        x: b.x, y: b.y, vx: b.vx, vy: b.vy, r: b.r


      }));


    },


    addBall(x, y, vx = 0, vy = 0, r) {


      const radius = (typeof r === 'number' && r > 0) ? r : 10 + Math.random() * 8;


      balls.push({


        x: x,


        y: y,


        vx: vx,


        vy: vy,


        r: radius,


        color: `hsl(${Math.random() * 360}, 70%, 60%)`


      });


      return balls[balls.length - 1];


    },


    clear() {


      balls.length = 0;


      draw();


    },


    step(dt = 1) {


      simulate(dt);


      draw();


    }


  };





  // ---------- Physics ----------


  function simulate(dt) {


    // Substepping to prevent tunneling at high velocities.


    // We pick the number of substeps based on the fastest ball.


    let maxSpeed = 0;


    for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {


      const s = Math.abs(balls[i].vx) + Math.abs(balls[i].vy);


      if (s > maxSpeed) maxSpeed = s;


    }


    let steps = Math.ceil(maxSpeed / 6);


    if (steps < 1) steps = 1;


    if (steps > MAX_SUBSTEPS) steps = MAX_SUBSTEPS;


    const subDt = dt / steps;





    for (let s = 0; s < steps; s++) {


      for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {


        const b = balls[i];


        // Apply gravity


        b.vy += GRAVITY * subDt;


        // Air friction (very mild)


        b.vx *= AIR_FRICTION;


        b.vy *= AIR_FRICTION;





        // Move


        b.x += b.vx * subDt;


        b.y += b.vy * subDt;





        // Collide with walls — clamp first to keep the ball fully inside,


        // then reverse the perpendicular component with energy loss.


        if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


          b.x = b.r;


          if (b.vx < 0) b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


        } else if (b.x + b.r > W) {


          b.x = W - b.r;


          if (b.vx > 0) b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


        }


        if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


          b.y = b.r;


          if (b.vy < 0) b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


        } else if (b.y + b.r > H) {


          b.y = H - b.r;


          if (b.vy > 0) b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


        }


      }


    }


  }





  // ---------- Rendering ----------


  function draw() {


    // Background


    ctx.fillStyle = '#161b22';


    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);





    // Subtle grid for atmosphere


    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(48, 54, 61, 0.4)';


    ctx.lineWidth = 1;


    const grid = 25;


    ctx.beginPath();


    for (let x = grid; x < W; x += grid) {


      ctx.moveTo(x, 0); ctx.lineTo(x, H);


    }


    for (let y = grid; y < H; y += grid) {


      ctx.moveTo(0, y); ctx.lineTo(W, y);


    }


    ctx.stroke();





    // Balls


    for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {


      const b = balls[i];


      // Soft glow


      const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(


        b.x - b.r * 0.3, b.y - b.r * 0.3, b.r * 0.1,


        b.x, b.y, b.r


      );


      grad.addColorStop(0

<script>


(function() {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const W = canvas.width;


  const H = canvas.height;





  const GRAVITY = 0.4;


  const RESTITUTION = 0.85;





  let balls = [];


  let animating = false;


  let animId = null;





  const COLORS = [


    '#ff6b6b', '#ffd93d', '#6bcb77', '#4d96ff',


    '#ff6eb4', '#a66cff', '#00d2d3', '#ff9f43',


    '#ee5a24', '#0abde3', '#10ac84', '#f368e0'


  ];





  function randomColor() {


    return COLORS[Math.floor(Math.random() * COLORS.length)];


  }





  function createBall(x, y, vx, vy, r) {


    if (r === undefined || r === null) r = 12 + Math.random() * 10;


    return {


      x: x,


      y: y,


      vx: vx || 0,


      vy: vy || 0,


      r: r,


      color: randomColor()


    };


  }





  function draw() {


    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);





    // Draw subtle grid


    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.03)';


    ctx.lineWidth = 1;


    for (let i = 0; i < W; i += 50) {


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.moveTo(i, 0);


      ctx.lineTo(i, H);


      ctx.stroke();


    }


    for (let i = 0; i < H; i += 50) {


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.moveTo(0, i);


      ctx.lineTo(W, i);


      ctx.stroke();


    }





    // Draw balls


    for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {


      const b = balls[i];





      // Shadow


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.arc(b.x + 2, b.y + 2, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)';


      ctx.fill();





      // Ball with gradient


      const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(


        b.x - b.r * 0.3, b.y - b.r * 0.3, b.r * 0.1,


        b.x, b.y, b.r


      );


      grad.addColorStop(0, '#ffffff');


      grad.addColorStop(0.3, b.color);


      grad.addColorStop(1, darkenColor(b.color, 0.4));





      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


      ctx.fillStyle = grad;


      ctx.fill();





      // Highlight


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.arc(b.x - b.r * 0.25, b.y - b.r * 0.25, b.r * 0.3, 0, Math.PI * 2);


      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.25)';


      ctx.fill();


    }


  }





  function darkenColor(hex, factor) {


    let r = parseInt(hex.slice(1, 3), 16);


    let g = parseInt(hex.slice(3, 5), 16);


    let b = parseInt(hex.slice(5, 7), 16);


    r = Math.floor(r * (1 - factor));


    g = Math.floor(g * (1 - factor));


    b = Math.floor(b * (1 - factor));


    return '#' + r.toString(16).padStart(2, '0') + g.toString(16).padStart(2, '0') + b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0');


  }





  function stepPhysics(dt) {


    if (dt === undefined || dt === null) dt = 1;





    for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {


      const b = balls[i];





      // Apply gravity


      b.vy += GRAVITY * dt;





      // Update position


      b.x += b.vx * dt;


      b.y += b.vy * dt;





      // Bounce off left wall


      if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


        b.x = b.r;


        b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


      }





      // Bounce off right wall


      if (b.x + b.r > W) {


        b.x = W - b.r;


        b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


      }





      // Bounce off top wall


      if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


        b.y = b.r;


        b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


      }





      // Bounce off bottom wall (floor)


      if (b.y + b.r > H) {


        b.y = H - b.r;


        b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


      }





      // Final clamp to ensure never outside


      b.x = Math.max(b.r, Math.min(W - b.r, b.x));


      b.y = Math.max(b.r, Math.min(H - b.r, b.y));


    }


  }

  <canvas id="sim" width="500" height="500"></canvas>


  <div class="controls">


    <button id="drop">Drop Ball</button>


    <button id="play">Pause</button>


    <button id="clear">Clear</button>


  </div>


  <p class="hint">Click the canvas to add a ball.</p>





  <script>


    (function() {


      const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


      const w = canvas.width;


      const h = canvas.height;





      const GRAVITY = 0.3;


      const REST = 0.85;





      let ballsList = [];


      let playing = true;


      let animationId = null;





      function draw() {


        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);


        for (const b of ballsList) {


          ctx.beginPath();


          ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


          ctx.fillStyle = b.color;


          ctx.fill();


        }


      }





      function step(dt = 1) {


        for (const b of ballsList) {


          b.vy += GRAVITY * dt;


          b.x += b.vx * dt;


          b.y += b.vy * dt;





          if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


            b.x = b.r;


            b.vx = Math.abs(b.vx) * REST;


          } else if (b.x + b.r > w) {


            b.x = w - b.r;


            b.vx = -Math.abs(b.vx) * REST;


          }





          if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


            b.y = b.r;


            b.vy = Math.abs(b.vy) * REST;


          } else if (b.y + b.r > h) {


            b.y = h - b.r;


            b.vy = -Math.abs(b.vy) * REST;


          }


        }


        draw();


      }





      window.sim = {


        w: w,


        h: h,


        balls: function() {


          return ballsList.map(b => ({


            x: b.x,


            y: b.y,


            vx: b.vx,


            vy: b.vy,


            r: b.r


          }));


        },


        addBall: function(x, y, vx = 0, vy = 0, r) {


          const radius = (typeof r === 'number') ? r : (12 + Math.random() * 8);


          ballsList.push({


            x: x,


            y: y,


            vx: vx,


            vy: vy,


            r: radius,


            color: `hsl(${Math.floor(Math.random() * 360)}, 85%, 65%)`


          });


          draw();


        },


        clear: function() {


          ballsList = [];


          draw();


        },


        step: step


      };





      canvas.addEventListener('click', (e) => {


        const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();


        const scaleX = canvas.width / rect.width;


        const scaleY = canvas.height / rect.height;


        const x = (e.clientX - rect.left) * scaleX;


        const y = (e.clientY - rect.top) * scaleY;


        window.sim.addBall(x, y, 0, 0);


      });





      const playBtn = document.getElementById('play');


      playBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {


        playing = !playing;


        playBtn.textContent = playing ? 'Pause' : 'Play';


        if (playing) {


          loop();


        } else if (animationId) {


          cancelAnimationFrame(animationId);


        }


      });





      document.getElementById('drop').addEventListener('click', () => {


        const x = 20 + Math.random() * (w - 40);


        window.sim.addBall(x, 30, 0, 0);


      });





      document.getElementById('clear').addEventListener('click', () => {


        window.sim.clear();


      });





      function loop() {


        if (!playing) return;


        step(1);


        animationId = requestAnimationFrame(loop);


      }





      loop();


    })();


  </script>


</body>


</html>

    function addBall(x = W / 2, y = 40, vx = 0, vy = 0, r = DEFAULT_RADIUS) {


      const radius = r !== undefined ? Number(r) : DEFAULT_RADIUS;


      let posX = Number(x !== undefined ? x : W / 2);

      posY = Math.max(radius, Math.min(H - radius, posY));

        x: posX,

        vx: Number(vx || 0),


        vy: Number(vy || 0),

        color: getRandomColor()


      };


      balls.push(ball);


      draw();

    }





    function clear() {


      balls = [];


      draw();


    }





    function step(dt = 1) {


      for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {


        const b = balls[i];





        // Apply gravity


        b.vy += GRAVITY * dt;





        // Advance position


        b.x += b.vx * dt;


        b.y += b.vy * dt;





        // Left / Right Wall Collision


        if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


          b.x = b.r;


          b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


        } else if (b.x + b.r > W) {


          b.x = W - b.r;


          b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


        }





        // Top / Floor Collision


        if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


          b.y = b.r;


          b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


        } else if (b.y + b.r > H) {


          b.y = H - b.r;


          b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


        }


      }


      draw();


    }

    <button id="clear">Clear</button>


  </div>


  <div class="hint">Tip: clicking the canvas spawns a ball at the cursor. The ball is pulled down by gravity and bounces off the walls with a little energy loss.</div>





<script>


(function () {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');





  // Logical canvas size (matches the width/height attributes).


  const W = canvas.width;


  const H = canvas.height;





  // Physics constants (per unit dt where dt=1 is one "standard" step).


  const GRAVITY = 0.45;


  const RESTITUTION = 0.82;


  const AIR_FRICTION = 0.999;


  const FLOOR_FRICTION = 0.98;





  const palette = [


    '#ff5e7a', '#ffae3b', '#4dd2ff', '#7af0a3', '#b07bff', '#ffd166', '#5eead4'


  ];


  let colorIdx = 0;





  const balls = [];





  function rand(a, b) { return a + Math.random() * (b - a); }





  function makeBall(x, y, vx, vy, r) {


    if (r === undefined || r === null || !isFinite(r) || r <= 0) {


      r = rand(14, 26);


    }


    r = Math.max(4, r);


    const color = palette[colorIdx % palette.length];


    colorIdx++;


    return { x, y, vx, vy, r, color, rot: 0 };


  }





  function addBall(x, y, vx, vy, r) {


    if (typeof vx !== 'number' || !isFinite(vx)) vx = 0;


    if (typeof vy !== 'number' || !isFinite(vy)) vy = 0;


    const b = makeBall(x, y, vx, vy, r);


    // Clamp inside the canvas immediately so it can never start out-of-bounds.


    b.x = Math.max(b.r, Math.min(W - b.r, b.x));


    b.y = Math.max(b.r, Math.min(H - b.r, b.y));


    balls.push(b);


    return b;


  }





  function clearBalls() {


    balls.length = 0;


  }





  function step(dt) {


    if (dt === undefined) dt = 1;


    if (!isFinite(dt)) dt = 1;


    if (dt < 0) dt = 0;





    for (const b of balls) {


      // Gravity accelerates downward.


      b.vy += GRAVITY * dt;





      // Mild air friction so things settle eventually.


      const fric = Math.pow(AIR_FRICTION, dt);


      b.vx *= fric;


      b.vy *= fric;





      // Integrate position.


      b.x += b.vx * dt;


      b.y += b.vy * dt;





      // Left wall.


      if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


        b.x = b.r;


        if (b.vx < 0) b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


      }


      // Right wall.


      else if (b.x + b.r > W) {


        b.x = W - b.r;


        if (b.vx > 0) b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


      }





      // Ceiling.


      if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


        b.y = b.r;


        if (b.vy < 0) b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


      }


      // Floor.


      else if (b.y + b.r > H) {


        b.y = H - b.r;


        if (b.vy > 0) b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


        // Extra damping when in contact with the floor so balls come to rest.


        b.vx *= FLOOR_FRICTION;


      }





      // Visual spin hint tied to horizontal motion.


      b.rot += b.vx * 0.05 * dt;


    }





    draw();


  }





  function draw() {


    ctx.clea

<script>


(function () {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const W = canvas.width;


  const H = canvas.height;





  const GRAVITY = 0.35;


  const RESTITUTION = 0.82;


  const FRICTION = 0.998;





  /** @type {{x:number,y:number,vx:number,vy:number,r:number,hue:number}[]} */


  let balls = [];


  let playing = false;


  let rafId = 0;





  const COLORS = [


    200, 170, 140, 30, 350, 280, 50, 320


  ];





  function randomHue() {


    return COLORS[Math.floor(Math.random() * COLORS.length)];


  }





  function addBall(x, y, vx = 0, vy = 0, r) {


    const radius = r != null ? r : 12 + Math.random() * 16;


    balls.push({


      x: x,


      y: y,


      vx: vx,


      vy: vy,


      r: radius,


      hue: randomHue()


    });


    draw();


  }





  function clearBalls() {


    balls = [];


    draw();


  }





  function step(dt = 1) {


    const n = Math.max(1, Math.round(dt));


    for (let s = 0; s < n; s++) {


      for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {


        const b = balls[i];


        b.vy += GRAVITY;


        b.vx *= FRICTION;


        b.x += b.vx;


        b.y += b.vy;





        // Left edge


        if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


          b.x = b.r;


          b.vx = Math.abs(b.vx) * RESTITUTION;


        }


        // Right edge


        if (b.x + b.r > W) {


          b.x = W - b.r;


          b.vx = -Math.abs(b.vx) * RESTITUTION;


        }


        // Top edge


        if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


          b.y = b.r;


          b.vy = Math.abs(b.vy) * RESTITUTION;


        }


        // Bottom edge


        if (b.y + b.r > H) {


          b.y = H - b.r;


          b.vy = -Math.abs(b.vy) * RESTITUTION;


        }





        // Clamp again to guarantee no escape / tunneling


        if (b.x - b.r < 0) b.x = b.r;


        if (b.x + b.r > W) b.x = W - b.r;


        if (b.y - b.r < 0) b.y = b.r;


        if (b.y + b.r > H) b.y = H - b.r;


      }


    }


    draw();


  }





  function draw() {


    ctx.fillStyle = '#16161f';


    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);





    // subtle grid


    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.03)';


    ctx.lineWidth = 1;


    for (let g = 40; g < W; g += 40) {


      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(g, 0); ctx.lineTo(g, H); ctx.stroke();


    }


    for (let g = 40; g < H; g += 40) {


      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, g); ctx.lineTo(W, g); ctx.stroke();


    }





    for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {


      const b = balls[i];


      const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(


        b.x - b.r * 0.35, b.y - b.r * 0.35, b.r * 0.1,


        b.x, b.y, b.r


      );


      grad.addColorStop(0, `hsl(${b.hue}, 85%, 72%)`);


      grad.addColorStop(0.55, `hsl(${b.hue}, 75%, 55%)`);


      grad.addColorStop(1, `hsl(${b.hue}, 70%, 38%)`);





      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


      ctx.fillStyle = grad;


      ctx.fill();





      // highlight


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.ellipse(


        b.x - b.r * 0.28, b.y - b.r * 0.32,


        b.r * 0.28, b.r * 0.18,


        -0.4, 0, Math.PI * 2


      );


      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.28)';


      ctx.fill();


    }


  }

    if (!playing) retu

<script>


(function () {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const W = canvas.width;


  const H = canvas.height;


  const GRAVITY = 0.45;


  const REST = 0.82;


  const DEFAULT_R = 18;


  const PALETTE = ['#5ee0a0', '#6aa8ff', '#ffd166', '#ff6b7a', '#c084fc', '#67e8f9'];





  const balls = [];


  let playing = true;


  let raf = 0;





  function clampBall(b) {


    if (b.r < 2) b.r = 2;


    const maxR = Math.min(W, H) / 2 - 0.5;


    if (b.r > maxR) b.r = maxR;


    if (b.x < b.r) b.x = b.r;


    if (b.x > W - b.r) b.x = W - b.r;


    if (b.y < b.r) b.y = b.r;


    if (b.y > H - b.r) b.y = H - b.r;


  }





  function addBall(x, y, vx, vy, r) {


    const ball = {


      x: Number(x),


      y: Number(y),


      vx: vx == null ? 0 : Number(vx),


      vy: vy == null ? 0 : Number(vy),


      r: r == null ? DEFAULT_R : Number(r),


      color: PALETTE[(Math.random() * PALETTE.length) | 0]


    };


    if (!isFinite(ball.x)) ball.x = W / 2;


    if (!isFinite(ball.y)) ball.y = ball.r;


    if (!isFinite(ball.vx)) ball.vx = 0;


    if (!isFinite(ball.vy)) ball.vy = 0;


    if (!isFinite(ball.r) || ball.r <= 0) ball.r = DEFAULT_R;


    clampBall(ball);


    balls.push(ball);


    draw();


    return ball;


  }





  function clearBalls() {


    balls.length = 0;


    draw();


  }





  function collide(b) {


    if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


      b.x = b.r;


      b.vx = Math.abs(b.vx) * REST;


    } else if (b.x + b.r > W) {


      b.x = W - b.r;


      b.vx = -Math.abs(b.vx) * REST;


    }


    if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


      b.y = b.r;


      b.vy = Math.abs(b.vy) * REST;


    } else if (b.y + b.r > H) {


      b.y = H - b.r;


      b.vy = -Math.abs(b.vy) * REST;


    }


  }





  function step(dt) {


    if (dt == null) dt = 1;


    dt = Number(dt);


    if (!isFinite(dt)) dt = 1;


    for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {


      const b = balls[i];


      b.vy += GRAVITY * dt;


      b.x += b.vx * dt;


      b.y += b.vy * dt;


      collide(b);


      clampBall(b);


    }


    draw();


  }

    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);


    ctx.save();

    ctx.lineWidth = 1;

      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.moveTo(g + 0.5, 0);


      ctx.lineTo(g + 0.5, H);


      ctx.stroke();


    }


    for (let g = 40; g < H; g += 40) {


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.moveTo(0, g + 0.5);


      ctx.lineTo(W, g + 0.5);


      ctx.stroke();


    }


    ctx.restore();

        b.x - b.r * 0.35, b.y - b.r * 0.4, b.r * 0.1,


        b.x, b.y, b.r


      );

      grd.addColorStop(0.18, b.color

<script>


(function() {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const GRAVITY = 0.5;


  const RESTITUTION = 0.8;





  let balls = [];


  let playing = true;


  let rafId = null;





  function resize() {


    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();


    canvas.width = Math.max(400, Math.floor(rect.width));


    canvas.height = Math.max(400, Math.floor(rect.height));


  }





  function defaultRadius() {


    return 10 + Math.random() * 10;


  }





  function addBall(x, y, vx = 0, vy = 0, r) {


    const radius = r == null ? defaultRadius() : +r;


    const ball = {


      x: +x,


      y: +y,


      vx: +vx,


      vy: +vy,


      r: radius,


      hue: Math.random() * 360


    };


    ball.x = Math.max(ball.r, Math.min(window.sim.w - ball.r, ball.x));


    ball.y = Math.max(ball.r, Math.min(window.sim.h - ball.r, ball.y));


    balls.push(ball);


    draw();


    return ball;


  }





  function clear() {


    balls = [];


    draw();


  }





  function step(dt = 1) {


    dt = +dt || 1;


    const w = window.sim.w;


    const h = window.sim.h;


    for (const b of balls) {


      b.vy += GRAVITY * dt;


      b.x += b.vx * dt;


      b.y += b.vy * dt;





      if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


        b.x = b.r;


        b.vx *= -RESTITUTION;


      } else if (b.x + b.r > w) {


        b.x = w - b.r;


        b.vx *= -RESTITUTION;


      }





      if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


        b.y = b.r;


        b.vy *= -RESTITUTION;


      } else if (b.y + b.r > h) {


        b.y = h - b.r;


        b.vy *= -RESTITUTION;


      }


    }


    draw();


  }





  function ballsSnapshot() {


    return balls.map(b => ({ x: b.x, y: b.y, vx: b.vx, vy: b.vy, r: b.r }));


  }





  function draw() {


    ctx.fillStyle = '#1f2937';


    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);





    ctx.strokeStyle = '#374151';


    ctx.lineWidth = 1;


    ctx.beginPath();


    for (let i = 0; i < canvas.width; i += 50) {


      ctx.moveTo(i, 0);


      ctx.lineTo(i, canvas.height);


    }


    for (let j = 0; j < canvas.height; j += 50) {


      ctx.moveTo(0, j);


      ctx.lineTo(canvas.width, j);


    }


    ctx.stroke();





    for (const b of balls) {


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


      const gradient = ctx.createRadialGradient(


        b.x - b.r * 0.3, b.y - b.r * 0.3, b.r * 0.1,


        b.x, b.y, b.r


      );


      gradient.addColorStop(0, `hsl(${b.hue}, 100%, 75%)`);


      gradient.addColorStop(1, `hsl(${b.hue}, 80%, 55%)`);


      ctx.fillStyle = gradient;


      ctx.fill();


      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.4)';


      ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;


      ctx.stroke();


    }


  }





  function loop() {


    if (!playing) return;


    step(1);


    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(loop);


  }





  window.sim = {


    get w() { return canvas.width; },


    get h() { return canvas.height; },


    balls: ballsSnapshot,


    addBall,


    clear,


    step


  };





  const dropBtn = document.getElementById('drop');


  const playBtn = document.getElementById('play');


  const clearBtn = document.getElementById('clear');





  dropBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {


    addBall(window.sim.w / 2, 50, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 6, 0);


  });





  playBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {


    playing = !playing;


    playBtn.textContent = playing ? 'Pause' : 'Play';


    if (playing) loop();


    else cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);


  });





  clearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {


    clear();


  });

    const rect = canva

<script>


(function(){


  const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const W = canvas.width;


  const H = canvas.height;





  const GRAVITY = 0.5;      // acceleration per step


  const RESTITUTION = 0.75; // energy retained on bounce


  const FRICTION = 0.999;   // slight air drag





  const colors = ['#6c8dff', '#ff6c8d', '#6cffb0', '#ffd76c', '#c46cff', '#6cf0ff'];





  let balls = [];


  let playing = true;


  let rafId = null;





  function randColor() {


    return colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * colors.length)];


  }





  function makeBall(x, y, vx = 0, vy = 0, r) {


    if (r === undefined || r === null) {


      r = 12 + Math.random() * 14;


    }


    return {


      x: x, y: y, vx: vx, vy: vy, r: r,


      color: randColor()


    };


  }





  function addBall(x, y, vx = 0, vy = 0, r) {


    // clamp initial position inside canvas so it doesn't spawn out of bounds


    const rr = (r === undefined || r === null) ? 12 + Math.random() * 14 : r;


    const cx = Math.min(Math.max(x, rr), W - rr);


    const cy = Math.min(Math.max(y, rr), H - rr);


    const b = makeBall(cx, cy, vx, vy, rr);


    balls.push(b);


    draw();


    return b;


  }





  function clearBalls() {


    balls.length = 0;


    draw();


  }





  function stepPhysics(dt) {


    if (dt === undefined) dt = 1;


    for (const b of balls) {


      // gravity


      b.vy += GRAVITY * dt;





      // mild drag


      b.vx *= FRICTION;


      b.vy *= FRICTION;





      // integrate


      b.x += b.vx * dt;


      b.y += b.vy * dt;





      // floor


      if (b.y + b.r > H) {


        b.y = H - b.r;


        if (b.vy > 0) b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


        if (Math.abs(b.vy) < 0.3) b.vy = 0;


      }


      // ceiling


      if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


        b.y = b.r;


        if (b.vy < 0) b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


      }


      // right wall


      if (b.x + b.r > W) {


        b.x = W - b.r;


        if (b.vx > 0) b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


      }


      // left wall


      if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


        b.x = b.r;


        if (b.vx < 0) b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


      }





      // final safety clamp - never allow tunneling / escape


      if (b.x < b.r) b.x = b.r;


      if (b.x > W - b.r) b.x = W - b.r;


      if (b.y < b.r) b.y = b.r;


      if (b.y > H - b.r) b.y = H - b.r;


    }


    draw();


  }





  function draw() {


    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);





    // subtle grid background


    ctx.save();


    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.03)';


    ctx.lineWidth = 1;


    for (let gx = 0; gx < W; gx += 40) {


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.moveTo(gx, 0);


      ctx.lineTo(gx, H);


      ctx.stroke();


    }

      ctx.lineTo(W, gy);


      ctx.stroke();

        b.x, b.y, b.r


      );


      grad.addColorStop(0, '
