<script>


(function() {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const W = canvas.width;


  const H = canvas.height;





  const GRAVITY = 0.4;


  const RESTITUTION = 0.78;


  const FRICTION = 0.995;


  const MIN_R = 10;


  const MAX_R = 28;





  let _balls = [];


  let animRunning = true;


  let rafId = null;


  let lastTime = null;





  const COLORS = [


    '#f472b6', '#818cf8', '#34d399', '#fbbf24',


    '#60a5fa', '#a78bfa', '#f87171', '#4ade80',


    '#fb923c', '#38bdf8'


  ];


  let colorIdx = 0;





  function randomColor() {


    const c = COLORS[colorIdx % COLORS.length];


    colorIdx++;


    return c;


  }





  function addBall(x, y, vx = 0, vy = 0, r) {


    if (r === undefined || r === null) {


      r = MIN_R + Math.random() * (MAX_R - MIN_R);


    }


    // Clamp position inside canvas


    const cx = Math.min(Math.max(x, r), W - r);


    const cy = Math.min(Math.max(y, r), H - r);


    _balls.push({


      x: cx, y: cy,


      vx: vx, vy: vy,


      r: r,


      color: randomColor()


    });


  }





  function clear() {


    _balls = [];


    draw();


  }





  function balls() {


    return _balls.map(b => ({ x: b.x, y: b.y, vx: b.vx, vy: b.vy, r: b.r }));


  }





  function step(dt = 1) {


    for (let b of _balls) {


      // Apply gravity


      b.vy += GRAVITY * dt;





      // Apply friction (horizontal damping in air)


      b.vx *= Math.pow(FRICTION, dt);





      // Move


      b.x += b.vx * dt;


      b.y += b.vy * dt;





      // Bounce off left wall


      if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


        b.x = b.r;


        b.vx = Math.abs(b.vx) * RESTITUTION;


      }


      // Bounce off right wall


      if (b.x + b.r > W) {


        b.x = W - b.r;


        b.vx = -Math.abs(b.vx) * RESTITUTION;


      }


      // Bounce off top


      if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


        b.y = b.r;


        b.vy = Math.abs(b.vy) * RESTITUTION;


      }


      // Bounce off bottom (floor)


      if (b.y + b.r > H) {


        b.y = H - b.r;


        b.vy = -Math.abs(b.vy) * RESTITUTION;


        // Extra horizontal friction on floor contact


        b.vx *= 0.98;


      }





      // Hard clamp to stay in bounds (safety net against tunneling)


      b.x = Math.min(Math.max(b.x, b.r), W - b.r);


      b.y = Math.min(Math.max(b.y, b.r), H - b.r);


    }


    draw();


  }





  function draw() {


    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);





    // Background


    ctx.fillStyle = '#0f0f23';


    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);





    // Grid lines (subtle)


    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.03)';


    ctx.lineWidth = 1;


    for (let gx = 0; gx < W; gx += 50) {


      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(gx, 0); ctx.lineTo(gx, H); ctx.stroke();


    }


    for (let gy = 0; gy < H; gy += 50) {


      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, gy); ctx.lineTo(W, gy); ctx.stroke();


    }





    for (let b of _balls) {


      // Shadow


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.arc(b.x + 2, b.y + 3, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.35)';


      ctx.fill();





      // Ball gradient


      const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(


        b.x - b.r * 0.3, b.y - b.r * 0.3, b.r * 0.1,


        b.x, b.y, b.r


      );


      grad.addColorStop(0, lighten(b.color, 0.6));


      grad.addColorStop(0.5, b.color);


      grad.addColorStop(1, darken(b.color, 0.5));





      ctx.beginPath();

```html
<script>


(function () {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const W = canvas.width;


  const H = canvas.height;





  const GRAVITY = 0.5;


  const RESTITUTION = 0.78;





  const COLORS = [


    '#ff6b6b', '#feca57', '#48dbfb', '#1dd1a1',


    '#ff9ff3', '#54a0ff', '#f368e0', '#00d2d3'


  ];





  let balls = [];


  let running = true;


  let colorIdx = 0;





  function makeBall(x, y, vx, vy, r) {


    r = r || (12 + Math.random() * 14);


    x = Math.min(Math.max(x, r), W - r);


    y = Math.min(Math.max(y, r), H - r);


    const color = COLORS[colorIdx % COLORS.length];


    colorIdx++;


    return { x, y, vx: vx || 0, vy: vy || 0, r, color };


  }





  function addBall(x, y, vx, vy, r) {


    const b = makeBall(x, y, vx, vy, r);


    balls.push(b);


    draw();


    return b;


  }





  function clearBalls() {


    balls = [];


    draw();


  }





  function step(dt) {


    dt = dt === undefined ? 1 : dt;


    for (const b of balls) {


      b.vy += GRAVITY * dt;


      b.x += b.vx * dt;


      b.y += b.vy * dt;





      if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


        b.x = b.r;


        b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


      } else if (b.x + b.r > W) {


        b.x = W - b.r;


        b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


      }





      if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


        b.y = b.r;


        b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


      } else if (b.y + b.r > H) {


        b.y = H - b.r;


        b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


      }


    }


    draw();


  }





  function draw() {


    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);





    for (const b of balls) {


      ctx.beginPath();


      const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(


        b.x - b.r * 0.35, b.y - b.r * 0.35, b.r * 0.1,


        b.x, b.y, b.r


      );


      grad.addColorStop(0, '#ffffff');


      grad.addColorStop(0.15, b.color);


      grad.addColorStop(1, b.color);


      ctx.fillStyle = grad;


      ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


      ctx.fill();





      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)';


      ctx.lineWidth = 1;


      ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


      ctx.stroke();


    }


  }





  function loop() {


    if (running) {


      step(1);


    }


    requestAnimationFrame(loop);


  }





  canvas.addEventListener('click', (e) => {


    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();


    const scaleX = W / rect.width;


    const scaleY = H / rect.height;


    const x = (e.clientX - rect.left) * scaleX;


    const y = (e.clientY - rect.top) * scaleY;


    addBall(x, y, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 6, -2);


  });





  document.getElementById('drop').addEventListener('click', () => {


    addBall(


      W / 2 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 100,


      40 + Math.random() * 40,


      (Math.random() - 0.5) * 8,


      0


    );


  });





  const playBtn = document.getElementById('play');


  playBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {


    running = !running;


    playBtn.textContent = running ? 'Pause' : 'Play';


    playBtn.classList.toggle('paused', !running);


  });





  document.getElementById('clear').addEventListener('click', clearBalls);





  // seed a couple of balls


  addBall(W * 0.35, 60, 3, 0);


  addBall(W * 0.65, 100, -2, 0);





  window.sim = {


    w: W,


    h: H,


    balls: () => balls.map(b => ({ x: b.x, y: b.y, vx: b.vx, vy: b.vy, r: b.r })),


    addBall: (x, y, vx, vy, r) => {


      const b = addBall(x, y, vx, vy, r);


      return { x: b.x, y: b.y, vx: b.vx, vy: b.vy, r: b.r };


    },


    clear: clearBalls,


    step: step


  };





  draw();


  requestAnimationFrame(loop);


})();


</script>
```

    (() => {


      const canvas = document.getElementById("sim");


      const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");


      const W = canvas.width, H = canvas.height;


      const balls = [];


      const gravity = 0.42;


      const restitution = 0.82;


      let playing = true, lastTime = performance.now();





      function color() {


        return `hsl(${Math.floor(Math.random() * 360)}, 88%, 64%)`;


      }





      function constrain(ball) {


        const r = Math.min(Math.max(2, ball.r), W / 2, H / 2);


        ball.r = r;


        ball.x = Math.max(r, Math.min(W - r, ball.x));


        ball.y = Math.max(r, Math.min(H - r, ball.y));


      }





      function addBall(x, y, vx = 0, vy = 0, r = 15) {


        const ball = {


          x: Number.isFinite(x) ? x : W / 2,


          y: Number.isFinite(y) ? y : 35,


          vx: Number.isFinite(vx) ? vx : 0,


          vy: Number.isFinite(vy) ? vy : 0,


          r: Number.isFinite(r) ? r : 15,


          color: color()


        };


        constrain(ball);


        balls.push(ball);


        draw();


        return ball;


      }





      function advance(dt = 1) {


        dt = Number.isFinite(dt) ? Math.max(0, dt) : 1;


        // Small substeps keep collisions reliable even for large supplied dt.


        const count = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(dt / 0.5));


        const slice = dt / count;





        for (let n = 0; n < count; n++) {


          for (const b of balls) {


            b.vy += gravity * slice;


            b.x += b.vx * slice;


            b.y += b.vy * slice;





            if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


              b.x = b.r;


              b.vx = Math.abs(b.vx) * restitution;


            } else if (b.x + b.r > W) {


              b.x = W - b.r;


              b.vx = -Math.abs(b.vx) * restitution;


            }





            if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


              b.y = b.r;


              b.vy = Math.abs(b.vy) * restitution;


            } else if (b.y + b.r > H) {


              b.y = H - b.r;


              b.vy = -Math.abs(b.vy) * restitution;


              if (Math.abs(b.vy) < 0.18) b.vy = 0;


            }


          }


        }


        draw();


      }





      function draw() {


        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);





        ctx.strokeStyle = "#5272ad33";


        ctx.lineWidth = 1;

          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, i); ctx.lineTo(W, i); ctx.stroke();


        }





        for (const b of balls) {


          const glow = ctx.createRadialGradient(


            b.x - b.r * .32, b.y - b.r * .36, 1,


            b.x, b.y, b.r * 1.2


          );


          glow.addColorStop(0, "#ffffff");


          glow.addColorStop(.16, b.color);


          glow.addColorStop(1, "#00000044");


          ctx.beginPath();


          ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);

<script>


(function () {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const W = canvas.width;


  const H = canvas.height;





  const GRAVITY = 0.35;      // per step at dt=1


  const RESTITUTION = 0.8;   // energy loss on bounce


  const DEFAULT_R = 14;





  let balls = [];


  let playing = true;





  const COLORS = ['#ff6b6b', '#ffd93d', '#6bcB77', '#4d96ff', '#c77dff', '#ff9f45', '#43e6c7'];


  let colorIdx = 0;





  function makeColor() {


    const c = COLORS[colorIdx % COLORS.length];


    colorIdx++;


    return c;


  }





  function addBall(x, y, vx = 0, vy = 0, r) {


    if (r === undefined || r === null) r = DEFAULT_R;


    r = Math.max(2, Math.min(r, Math.min(W, H) / 2 - 1));


    // clamp initial position inside bounds


    x = Math.max(r, Math.min(W - r, x));


    y = Math.max(r, Math.min(H - r, y));


    const b = { x, y, vx, vy, r, color: makeColor() };


    balls.push(b);


    draw();


    return b;


  }





  function clear() {


    balls = [];


    draw();


  }





  function step(dt = 1) {


    for (const b of balls) {


      // gravity


      b.vy += GRAVITY * dt;





      // integrate


      b.x += b.vx * dt;


      b.y += b.vy * dt;





      // left / right walls


      if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


        b.x = b.r;


        b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


      } else if (b.x + b.r > W) {


        b.x = W - b.r;


        b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


      }





      // top / bottom (floor & ceiling)


      if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


        b.y = b.r;


        b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


      } else if (b.y + b.r > H) {


        b.y = H - b.r;


        b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


      }


    }


    draw();


  }





  function draw() {


    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);


    for (const b of balls) {


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


      const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(


        b.x - b.r * 0.35, b.y - b.r * 0.35, b.r * 0.1,


        b.x, b.y, b.r


      );


      grad.addColorStop(0, '#ffffff');


      grad.addColorStop(0.25, b.color);


      grad.addColorStop(1, b.color);


      ctx.fillStyle = grad;


      ctx.fill();


      ctx.lineWidth = 1;


      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.25)';


      ctx.stroke();


    }


  }





  // animation loop


  function loop() {


    if (playing) step(1);


    requestAnimationFrame(loop);


  }


  requestAnimationFrame(loop);





  // canvas click -> add ball at cursor


  canvas.addEventListener('click', (e) => {


    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();


    const x = (e.clientX - rect.left) * (W / rect.width);


    const y = (e.clientY - rect.top) * (H / rect.height);


    const vx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 6;


    addBall(x, y, vx, 0, 10 + Math.random() * 12);


  });





  // buttons


  document.getElementById('drop').addEventListener('click', () => {


    const x = W * (0.25 + Math.random() * 0.5);


    addBall(x, 30, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4, 0, 10 + Math.random() * 12);


  });





  const playBtn = document.getElementById('play');

    playing = !playing;


    playBtn.textContent = playing ? 'Pause' : 'Play';


  });





  document.getElementById('clear').addEventListener('click', clear);

    balls() {

<script>


(function () {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const W = canvas.width;


  const H = canvas.height;





  const GRAVITY = 0.4;       // downward acceleration per step


  const RESTITUTION = 0.8;   // energy retained on bounce


  const FRICTION = 0.999;    // slight air drag


  const DEFAULT_R = 14;





  let balls = [];


  let running = true;





  const COLORS = ['#58a6ff', '#f778ba', '#3fb950', '#d29922', '#a371f7', '#ff7b72', '#39c5cf'];





  function makeBall(x, y, vx, vy, r) {


    r = (typeof r === 'number' && r > 0) ? r : DEFAULT_R;


    // keep spawn point inside the canvas


    x = Math.min(W - r, Math.max(r, x));


    y = Math.min(H - r, Math.max(r, y));


    return {


      x: x, y: y,


      vx: vx || 0, vy: vy || 0,


      r: r,


      color: COLORS[(Math.random() * COLORS.length) | 0]


    };


  }





  function stepBall(b, dt) {


    // gravity


    b.vy += GRAVITY * dt;





    // integrate


    b.vx *= FRICTION;


    b.x += b.vx * dt;


    b.y += b.vy * dt;





    // left / right walls


    if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


      b.x = b.r;


      b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


    } else if (b.x + b.r > W) {


      b.x = W - b.r;


      b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


    }





    // ceiling / floor


    if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


      b.y = b.r;


      b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


    } else if (b.y + b.r > H) {


      b.y = H - b.r;


      b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


    }


  }





  function step(dt) {


    if (typeof dt !== 'number' || !isFinite(dt) || dt <= 0) dt = 1;


    for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {


      stepBall(balls[i], dt);


    }


    draw();


  }





  function draw() {


    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);


    for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {


      const b = balls[i];


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


      ctx.fillStyle = b.color;


      ctx.shadowColor = b.color;


      ctx.shadowBlur = 12;


      ctx.fill();


      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;


      // highlight


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.arc(b.x - b.r * 0.3, b.y - b.r * 0.3, b.r * 0.35, 0, Math.PI * 2);


      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)';


      ctx.fill();


    }


  }





  function loop() {


    if (running) step(1);


    requestAnimationFrame(loop);


  }





  // ---- Programmatic API ----


  window.sim = {


    w: W,


    h: H,


    balls: function () {


      return balls.map(function (b) {


        return { x: b.x, y: b.y, vx: b.vx, vy: b.vy, r: b.r };


      });


    },


    addBall: function (x, y, vx, vy, r) {


      const b = makeBall(x, y, vx, vy, r);


      balls.push(b);


      draw();


      return b;


    },


    clear: function () {


      balls = [];


      draw();


    },


    step: step


  };

    window.sim.addBall(x, DEFAULT_R + 4, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 6, 0);


  });





  const playBtn = document.getElementById('play');


  playBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {


    running =

      "use strict";





      const canvas = document.getElementById("sim");


      const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");

      const playButton = document.getElementById("play");


      const clearButton = document.getElementById("clear");





      const W = canvas.width;


      const H = canvas.height;


      const gravity = 0.42;


      const restitution = 0.82;


      const state = [];


      let running = true;


      let lastTime = performance.now();


      let hue = 185;





      function finite(value, fallback) {


        value = Number(value);


        return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : fallback;


      }





      function draw() {


        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);





        const background = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H);


        background.addColorStop(0, "#11182d");


        background.addColorStop(1, "#090d18");


        ctx.fillStyle = background;


        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);





        ctx.strokeStyle = "#7986b51c";


        ctx.lineWidth = 1;


        for (let x = 30; x < W; x += 30) {


          ctx.beginPath();


          ctx.moveTo(x, 0);


          ctx.lineTo(x, H);


          ctx.stroke();


        }


        for (let y = 30; y < H; y += 30) {


          ctx.beginPath();


          ctx.moveTo(0, y);


          ctx.lineTo(W, y);


          ctx.stroke();


        }





        for (const ball of state) {


          ctx.save();


          ctx.shadowColor = `hsla(${ball.hue}, 95%, 65%, .65)`;


          ctx.shadowBlur = 16;





          const shine = ctx.createRadialGradient(


            ball.x - ball.r * .32,


            ball.y - ball.r * .38,


            ball.r * .08,


            ball.x,


            ball.y,


            ball.r


          );


          shine.addColorStop(0, "#ffffff");


          shine.addColorStop(.16, `hsl(${ball.hue}, 95%, 75%)`);


          shine.addColorStop(.65, `hsl(${ball.hue}, 82%, 55%)`);


          shine.addColorStop(1, `hsl(${ball.hue}, 78%, 30%)`);





          ctx.beginPath();


          ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, ball.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


          ctx.fillStyle = shine;


          ctx.fill();


          ctx.restore();


        }


      }





      function addBall(x, y, vx = 0, vy = 0, r) {


        const radius = Math.max(4, Math.min(


          finite(r, 12 + Math.random() * 10),


          Math.min(W, H) / 2


        ));





        const ball = {


          x: Math.max(radius, Math.min(W - radius, finite(x, W / 2))),


          y: Math.max(radius, Math.min(H - radius, finite(y, radius))),

<script>


(function () {


  "use strict";





  var canvas = document.getElementById("sim");


  var ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");


  var W = canvas.width;


  var H = canvas.height;





  var GRAVITY = 0.5;       // px per step^2


  var RESTITUTION = 0.82;  // energy kept per bounce


  var FRICTION = 0.995;    // mild horizontal drag


  var MAX_SPEED = 60;      // hard cap so nothing can tunnel





  var balls = [];


  var running = true;


  var nextHue = 200;





  function clampNum(v, lo, hi) {


    return v < lo ? lo : (v > hi ? hi : v);


  }





  function addBall(x, y, vx, vy, r) {


    var radius = (typeof r === "number" && isFinite(r) && r > 0) ? r : (8 + Math.random() * 12);


    radius = clampNum(radius, 2, Math.min(W, H) / 2 - 1);


    var b = {


      x: clampNum(Number(x) || 0, radius, W - radius),


      y: clampNum(Number(y) || 0, radius, H - radius),


      vx: Number(vx) || 0,


      vy: Number(vy) || 0,


      r: radius,


      hue: nextHue


    };


    nextHue = (nextHue + 47) % 360;


    balls.push(b);


    return b;


  }





  function clearBalls() {


    balls.length = 0;


    draw();


  }





  // Integrate one ball by a sub-step of length h, then resolve edges.


  function integrate(b, h) {


    b.vy += GRAVITY * h;


    b.vx *= Math.pow(FRICTION, h);





    b.vx = clampNum(b.vx, -MAX_SPEED, MAX_SPEED);


    b.vy = clampNum(b.vy, -MAX_SPEED, MAX_SPEED);





    b.x += b.vx * h;


    b.y += b.vy * h;





    // Left / right walls


    if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


      b.x = b.r;


      if (b.vx < 0) b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


    } else if (b.x + b.r > W) {


      b.x = W - b.r;


      if (b.vx > 0) b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


    }





    // Ceiling / floor


    if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


      b.y = b.r;


      if (b.vy < 0) b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


    } else if (b.y + b.r > H) {


      b.y = H - b.r;


      if (b.vy > 0) b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


    }





    // Absolute safety clamp — a ball can never leave the canvas.


    b.x = clampNum(b.x, b.r, W - b.r);


    b.y = clampNum(b.y, b.r, H - b.r);


  }





  function step(dt) {


    var total = (typeof dt === "number" && isFinite(dt)) ? dt : 1;


    if (total <= 0) { draw(); return; }





    // Sub-step so fast balls cannot pass through an edge in one update.


    var subs = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / 0.5));


    var h = total / subs;





    for (var s = 0; s < subs; s++) {


      for (var i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {


        integrate(balls[i], h);


      }


    }


    draw();


  }





  function draw() {


    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);





    // backdrop


    ctx.fillStyle = "#11161d";


    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);





    // subtle grid


    ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.04)";


    ctx.lineWidth = 1;


    ctx.beginPath();


    for (var g = 40; g < W; g += 40) {


      ctx.moveTo(g + 0.5, 0);


      ctx.lineTo(g + 0.5, H);


    }


    for (var k = 40; k < H; k += 40) {

<script>


(function () {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const W = canvas.width;


  const H = canvas.height;





  const GRAVITY = 0.35;       // per step, per unit dt


  const RESTITUTION = 0.82;   // energy kept on bounce


  const FRICTION = 0.995;     // mild air drag





  const COLORS = ['#6ea8fe', '#f56c8d', '#5ed3a8', '#f0c95e', '#b58cf5', '#5ecbe8'];


  let colorIdx = 0;





  let balls = [];


  let running = true;





  function addBall(x, y, vx = 0, vy = 0, r) {


    if (r == null) r = 8 + Math.random() * 14;


    r = Math.max(2, Math.min(r, Math.min(W, H) / 2 - 1));


    // Clamp spawn point fully inside the canvas


    x = Math.max(r, Math.min(W - r, x));


    y = Math.max(r, Math.min(H - r, y));


    const ball = { x, y, vx, vy, r, color: COLORS[colorIdx++ % COLORS.length] };


    balls.push(ball);


    draw();


    return ball;


  }





  function clear() {


    balls = [];


    draw();


  }





  function step(dt = 1) {


    // Sub-step large dt values so fast balls can't tunnel through edges


    let remaining = dt;


    while (remaining > 0) {


      const h = Math.min(remaining, 1);


      integrate(h);


      remaining -= h;


    }


    draw();


  }





  function integrate(h) {


    for (const b of balls) {


      b.vy += GRAVITY * h;


      b.vx *= Math.pow(FRICTION, h);


      b.x += b.vx * h;


      b.y += b.vy * h;





      // Floor


      if (b.y + b.r > H) {


        b.y = H - b.r;


        if (b.vy > 0) b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


        // kill tiny jitter so balls settle


        if (Math.abs(b.vy) < GRAVITY * 1.5) b.vy = 0;


      }


      // Ceiling


      if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


        b.y = b.r;


        if (b.vy < 0) b.vy = -b.vy * RESTITUTION;


      }


      // Right wall


      if (b.x + b.r > W) {


        b.x = W - b.r;


        if (b.vx > 0) b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


      }


      // Left wall


      if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


        b.x = b.r;


        if (b.vx < 0) b.vx = -b.vx * RESTITUTION;


      }


    }


  }





  function draw() {


    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);


    for (const b of balls) {


      const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(


        b.x - b.r * 0.35, b.y - b.r * 0.35, b.r * 0.1,


        b.x, b.y, b.r


      );


      grad.addColorStop(0, '#ffffff');


      grad.addColorStop(0.25, b.color);


      grad.addColorStop(1, shade(b.color));


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


      ctx.fillStyle = grad;


      ctx.fill();


    }


  }





  function shade(hex) {


    const n = parseInt(hex.slice(1), 16);


    const r = (n >> 16) & 255, g = (n >> 8) & 255, bl = n & 255;


    return `rgb(${r * 0.45 | 0}, ${g * 0.45 | 0}, ${bl * 0.45 | 0})`;


  }





  // --- UI wiring ---


  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => {


    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();


    const x = (e.clientX - rect.left) * (W / rect.width);


    const y = (e.clientY - rect.top) * (H / rect.height);


    addBall(x, y, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 6, -Math.random() * 3);


  });





  document.getElementById('drop').addEventListener('click', () => {


    addBall(


      W * (0.2 + Math.random() * 0.6),


      H * 0.15,


      (Math.random() - 0.5) * 5,


      0


    );

<script>


(function() {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  


  // Physics constants


  const GRAVITY = 0.5; 


  const RESTITUTION = 0.78; 


  const FRICTION = 1.0; 





  let balls = [];





  // Dimensions (in pixels) for physics logic - internal canvas resolution


  const W = parseInt(canvas.width); 


  const H = parseInt(canvas.height);





  function drawBall(ball) {


    ctx.beginPath();


    ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, ball.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


    


    // Fill based on index or random? Let's use consistent colors.


    const hue = (Math.floor(Math.random() * 360));


    ctx.fillStyle = `hsl(${hue}, 70%, 60%)`; 


    ctx.fill();


    


    // Stroke for contrast against dark background


    ctx.lineWidth = 2;


    ctx.strokeStyle = 'white';


    ctx.stroke();





    // Add a subtle highlight to make it look spherical (satisfying)


    ctx.beginPath();


    ctx.arc(ball.x - ball.r*0.3, ball.y - ball.r*0.3, ball.r/3, 0, Math.PI * 2);


    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.4)';


    ctx.fill();


  }





  // The required API implementation


  window.sim = {


    w: W, 


    h: H,


    


    balls() { return balls.slice(); },


    


    addBall(x, y, vx=0, vy=0, r) {


        if (r === undefined) r = Math.max(15, Math.floor(Math.random() * 20 + 10)); 


        // Ensure spawn inside bounds initially


        const safeX = Math.min(Math.max(r, x), W - r);


        const safeY = Math.min(Math.max(r, y), H - r);


        


        balls.push({ x: safeX, y: safeY, vx, vy, r });


    },





    clear() {


      balls = [];


      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H); // Clear canvas immediately on command


    },





    step(dt=1) {


        // Advance the simulation one step (and redraw)


        


        // Clear background for this frame


        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);


        


        balls.forEach(ball => {


            ball.vy += GRAVITY * dt;


            


            // Air friction to settle things down eventually


            ball.vx *= FRICTION; 


            ball.vy *= FRICTION;





            ball.x += ball.vx * dt;


            ball.y += ball.vy * dt;





            // Floor Collision (Bottom)


            if (ball.y + ball.r > H) {


                ball.y = H - ball.r; // Clamp inside


                ball.vy *= -RESTITUTION;

                ball.vx *= -RESTITUTION;


            } else if (ball.x - ball.r < 0) {


                ball.x = ball.r;     // Left Wall


                ball.vx *= -RESTITUTION;


            }





            drawBall(ball);


        });


    },


    


    get isRunning() { return running; } 


  };

  let running = true;


  let requestAnimFrameId = null;





  function runLoop() {


      if (!running) return;


      


      window.sim.step(1); 


      requestAnimFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(runLoop);


  }

<script>


(function() {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');





  // Canvas dimensions in pixels


  const w = canvas.width;


  const h = canvas.height;





  // Physics constants


  const GRAVITY = 600;        // pixels per second^2 (or per step at dt=1)


  const RESTITUTION = 0.85;   // energy loss on bounce (< 1)


  const FRICTION = 0.999;     // slight air resistance





  let balls = [];


  let running = true;





  // Color palette for balls


  const COLORS = [


    ['#e94560', '#c23152'],


    ['#f39c12', '#d68910'],


    ['#2ecc71', '#27ae60'],


    ['#3498db', '#2980b9'],


    ['#9b59b6', '#8e44ad'],


    ['#1abc9c', '#16a085'],


    ['#e74c3c', '#c0392b'],


    ['#f1c40f', '#d4ac0d'],


  ];





  function createBall(x, y, vx, vy, r) {


    const colorIdx = Math.floor(Math.random() * COLORS.length);


    return {


      x: x,


      y: y,


      vx: vx || 0,


      vy: vy || 0,


      r: r || (15 + Math.random() * 20), // radius between 15 and 35


      colorStart: COLORS[colorIdx][0],


      colorEnd: COLORS[colorIdx][1]


    };


  }





  function step(dt) {


    dt = dt || 1;


    


    for (let i = balls.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {


      const b = balls[i];


      


      // Apply gravity


      b.vy += GRAVITY * dt;


      


      // Apply friction


      b.vx *= FRICTION;


      b.vy *= FRICTION;


      


      // Update position


      b.x += b.vx * dt;


      b.y += b.vy * dt;


      


      // Boundary checks with clamping and bounce


      // Floor


      if (b.y + b.r > h) {


        b.y = h - b.r;


        b.vy = -Math.abs(b.vy) * RESTITUTION;


        // Stop micro-bouncing when near rest


        if (Math.abs(b.vy) < 5) b.vy = 0;


      }


      


      // Ceiling


      if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


        b.y = b.r;


        b.vy = Math.abs(b.vy) * RESTITUTION;


      }


      


      // Right wall


      if (b.x + b.r > w) {


        b.x = w - b.r;


        b.vx = -Math.abs(b.vx) * RESTITUTION;


      }


      


      // Left wall


      if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


        b.x = b.r;


        b.vx = Math.abs(b.vx) * RESTITUTION;


      }


    }


    


    draw();


  }





  function draw() {


    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);


    


    // Draw subtle grid


    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(69, 123, 157, 0.1)';


    ctx.lineWidth = 1;


    for (let x = 0; x < w; x += 50) {


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.moveTo(x, 0);


      ctx.lineTo(x, h);


      ctx.stroke();


    }


    for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 50) {


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.moveTo(0, y);


      ctx.lineTo(w, y);


      ctx.stroke();


    }


    


    // Draw each ball with a nice gradient


    for (const b of balls) {


      const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(


        b.x - b.r * 0.3, b.y - b.r * 0.3, b.r * 0.1,


        b.x, b.y, b.r


      );


      grad.addColorStop(0, lighten(b.colorStart, 40));


      grad.addColorStop(0.7, b.colorStart);


      grad.addColorStop(1, b.colorEnd);


      


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


      ctx.fillStyle = grad;


      ct

  function randomColor() {


    return COLORS[Math.floor(Math.random() * COLORS.length)];


  }





  function createBall(x, y, vx, vy, r) {


    return {


      x: x !== undefined ? x : W / 2 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 200,


      y: y !== undefined ? y : 40,


      vx: vx || 0,


      vy: vy || 0,


      r: r || (DEFAULT_RADIUS + Math.random() * 10 - 5),


      color: randomColor()


    };


  }





  function clamp(val, min, max) {


    return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, val));


  }





  function drawBall(ball) {


    const { x, y, r, color } = ball;





    // Glow


    ctx.save();


    ctx.shadowColor = color;


    ctx.shadowBlur = 15;





    // Main circle


    const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(x - r * 0.3, y - r * 0.3, r * 0.1, x, y, r);


    grad.addColorStop(0, '#ffffff');


    grad.addColorStop(0.3, color);


    grad.addColorStop(1, shadeColor(color, -40));





    ctx.beginPath();


    ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


    ctx.fillStyle = grad;


    ctx.fill();


    ctx.restore();





    // Highlight


    ctx.beginPath();


    ctx.arc(x - r * 0.25, y - r * 0.25, r * 0.35, 0, Math.PI * 2);


    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.25)';


    ctx.fill();


  }





  function shadeColor(hex, percent) {


    let num = parseInt(hex.replace('#', ''), 16);


    let r = Math.min(255, Math.max(0, (num >> 16) + percent));


    let g = Math.min(255, Math.max(0, ((num >> 8) & 0x00FF) + percent));


    let b = Math.min(255, Math.max(0, (num & 0x0000FF) + percent));


    return '#' + (0x1000000 + r * 0x10000 + g * 0x100 + b).toString(16).slice(1);


  }





  function draw() {


    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);

    }

    }

    ctx.lineWidth = 2;

    ctx.lineTo(W, H - 1);


    ctx.stroke();





    for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {


      drawBall(balls[i]);


    }


  }

    // dt in seconds

      b.vy += GRAVITY * dt;





      // Air friction


      b.vx *= Math.pow(AIR_FRICTION, dt * 60);


      b.vy *= Math.p

<script>


/**


 * PHYSICS PLAYGROUND ENGINE


 */


(function() {


    const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


    const btnDrop = document.getElementById('drop');


    const btnPlay = document.getElementById('play');


    const btnClear = document.getElementById('clear');





    const config = {


        gravity: 0.3,


        restitution: 0.85, // Energy loss on bounce


        colors: ['#FF5252', '#FF4081', '#E040FB', '#7C4DFF', '#536DFE', '#448AFF', '#40C4FF', '#18FFFF', '#64FFDA', '#69F0AE', '#B2FF59', '#EEFF41', '#FFFF00', '#FFD740', '#FFAB40', '#FF6E40']


    };





    const state = {


        balls: [],


        isPaused: false


    };





    // Physics Logic


    function updatePhysics(dt) {


        for (let i = 0; i < state.balls.length; i++) {


            const b = state.balls[i];





            // Apply Gravity


            b.vy += config.gravity * dt;





            // Update Position


            b.x += b.vx * dt;


            b.y += b.vy * dt;





            // Collision: Left/Right walls


            if (b.x - b.r < 0) {


                b.x = b.r;


                b.vx = Math.abs(b.vx) * config.restitution;


            } else if (b.x + b.r > canvas.width) {


                b.x = canvas.width - b.r;


                b.vx = -Math.abs(b.vx) * config.restitution;


            }





            // Collision: Top/Bottom walls


            if (b.y - b.r < 0) {


                b.y = b.r;


                b.vy = Math.abs(b.vy) * config.restitution;


            } else if (b.y + b.r > canvas.height) {


                b.y = canvas.height - b.r;


                b.vy = -Math.abs(b.vy) * config.restitution;


            }


        }


    }





    function render() {


        // Clear background


        ctx.fillStyle = '#1e1e1e';


        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);





        // Draw balls


        for (let i = 0; i < state.balls.length; i++) {


            const b = state.balls[i];


            ctx.beginPath();


            ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


            ctx.fillStyle = b.color || '#ffffff';


            ctx.fill();


            // Add a little shine for aesthetics


            ctx.shadowBlur = 10;


            ctx.shadowColor = b.color || '#ffffff';


            ctx.closePath();


        }


        // Reset shadow for next frame


        ctx.shadowBlur = 0;


    }





    /**


     * PUBLIC API - Required for grading


     */


    window.sim = {


        get w() { return canvas.width; },

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>


<html>


<head>


  <title>Bouncing Ball Playground</title>


  <style>


    body {


      background-color: #1e1e1e;


      margin: 0;


      overflow: hidden;


      display: flex;


      justify-content: center;


      align-items: center;


      height: 100vh;


    }





    #sim {


      border: 1px solid #555;


      background-color: #333;


    }





    button {


      background-color: #555;


      color: #eee;


      padding: 10px 20px;


      border: none;


      cursor: pointer;


      margin: 10px;


      transition: background-color 0.2s ease;


    }





    button:hover {


      background-color: #777;


    }


  </style>


</head>


<body>


  <canvas id="sim"></canvas>


  <button id="drop">Drop Ball</button>


  <button id="play">Play</button>


  <button id="clear">Clear</button>





  <script>


    const canvas = document.getElementById('sim');


    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


    window.w = canvas.width;


    window.h = canvas.height;





    window.sim = {


      w,


      h,


      balls: [],


      addBall(x, y, vx = 0, vy = 0, r = 10) {


        this.balls.push({ x, y, vx, vy, r });


      },


      clear() {


        this.balls = [];


      },


      step(dt = 1) {


        for (const ball of this.balls) {


          ball.vy += dt * 9.8; // Gravity





          // Bounce off floor


          if (ball.y + ball.r > window.h) {


            ball.y = window.h - ball.r;


            ball.vy *= -0.8; // Restitution (energy loss)


          }





          // Bounce off walls


          if (ball.x - ball.r < 0 || ball.x + ball.r > window.w) {


            ball.x = ball.r * Math.sign(ball.x - ball.r);


            ball.vx *= -1; // Reverse x velocity


          }


          if (ball.y - ball.r < 0 || ball.y + ball.r > window.h) {


              ball.y = ball.r * Math.sign(ball.y-ball.r);


              ball.vy *= -1;


          }


        }





        // Clear canvas


        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, window.w, window.h);





        // Draw balls


        for (const ball of this.balls) {


          ctx.beginPath();


          ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, ball.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);


          ctx.fillStyle = '#ffcc66';


          ctx.fill();


          ctx.closePath();


        }


      },


    };





    let playing = false;


    let lastTime = 0;





    document.getElementById('play').addEventListener('click', () => {


      playing = true;


    });





    document.getElementById('clear').addEventListener('click', () => {


      window.sim.clear();


    });





    canvas.addEventListener('click', (event) => {


      const x = event.clientX - window.w / 2;


      const y = event.clientY - window.h / 2;


      window.sim.addBall(x, y);


    });








    function animate() {


      let now = performance.now();


      let dt = (now - lastTime) / 1000;





      if (playing) {


        window.sim.step(dt);


      }





      lastTime = now;


      requestAnimationFrame(animate);


    }





    animate();


  </script>


</body>


</html>


```

Claude has no cost figure here, on purpose. Every Claude model is measured through the Claude Code CLI, which runs on a subscription — there is no per-token price to report, so any number would be invented. Every other row is what the provider billed, or list pricing on real token counts. We tried publishing a derived API-equivalent and measurement refuted it twice: the CLI sends its own instructions, reads its own files and carries its own conversation, so it consumed 16× more input per turn than the same model doing the same task through an API — a different agent, not a fixed overhead, and nothing you can subtract. Claude cost lands here when the full suite has been run through the API, not before.
Latest-per-model table ranks by score, then cost, then speed. Token counts come from provider usage fields. Outputs are verbatim transcripts, truncated for display — full text lives in runs/…/transcript.jsonl.
